# Ireland runtime registry. Mission capacity, route profiles, persistent spirits, leaders, Northern integration, achievements, and event gateways are owned here.
# Every helper is country-scoped and idempotent; no helper transfers territory or grants a core without the canonical review gate.

# Shared action scarcity charges. Action-specific base PP/CP and Action Burden
# remain authored in each decision; these two branches are the common dynamic
# surcharge applied by every action's live scarcity band.
ireland_apply_action_scarcity_severe_surcharge = {
	add_to_variable = { var = ireland_action_political_cost value = constant:ireland.action_surcharge_severe }
	add_to_variable = { var = ireland_action_command_cost value = constant:ireland.action_surcharge_severe }
	add_to_variable = { var = ireland_action_burden value = constant:ireland.action_burden_surcharge_severe }
	set_temp_variable = { var = ireland_action_scarcity_charge value = constant:ireland.action_surcharge_severe }
	multiply_temp_variable = { ireland_action_scarcity_charge = -1 }
	add_political_power = ireland_action_scarcity_charge
	add_command_power = ireland_action_scarcity_charge
}

ireland_apply_action_scarcity_watch_surcharge = {
	add_to_variable = { var = ireland_action_political_cost value = constant:ireland.action_surcharge_watch }
	add_to_variable = { var = ireland_action_command_cost value = constant:ireland.action_surcharge_watch }
	add_to_variable = { var = ireland_action_burden value = constant:ireland.action_burden_surcharge_watch }
	set_temp_variable = { var = ireland_action_scarcity_charge value = constant:ireland.action_surcharge_watch }
	multiply_temp_variable = { ireland_action_scarcity_charge = -1 }
	add_political_power = ireland_action_scarcity_charge
	add_command_power = ireland_action_scarcity_charge
}

ireland_update_neutrality_partner_depth = {
	# These are persistent high-water records, not a national cooperation meter.
	# Each named partner has four concrete channels. A focus-created channel is
	# administrative contact (1), a completed action is technical cooperation
	# (2), a completed mission or liaison is operational cooperation (3), and a
	# high-exposure operational record is undeclared co-belligerency (4).
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_british_channel_open check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_access value = constant:ireland.one compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_access value = constant:ireland.one } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_american_channel_open check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_access value = constant:ireland.one compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_access value = constant:ireland.one } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_german_channel_open check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_access value = constant:ireland.one compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_access value = constant:ireland.one } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_vatican_channel_open check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_access value = constant:ireland.one compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_access value = constant:ireland.one } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_neutral_states_channel_open check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_access value = constant:ireland.one compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_access value = constant:ireland.one } }

	# British channels: supply shipping, Plan W intelligence, access, and Blacksod weather.
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_renegotiate_a_british_supply_contract_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_shipping value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_shipping value = constant:ireland.two } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_deliver_a_british_supply_agreement_full_recorded check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_shipping value = constant:ireland.three compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_shipping value = constant:ireland.three } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_formalise_plan_w_liaison_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_intelligence value = constant:ireland.three compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_intelligence value = constant:ireland.three } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_share_a_controlled_weather_report_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_weather value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_weather value = constant:ireland.two } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_the_blacksod_station_reporting_full_recorded check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_weather value = constant:ireland.three compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_weather value = constant:ireland.three } }

	# American channels: procurement, intelligence contact, access, and diaspora relief.
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_send_an_american_procurement_mission_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_procurement value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_procurement value = constant:ireland.two } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_complete_an_american_procurement_mission_full_recorded check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_procurement value = constant:ireland.three compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_procurement value = constant:ireland.three } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_send_an_american_procurement_mission_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_intelligence value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_intelligence value = constant:ireland.two } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_mobilise_diaspora_relief_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_relief value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_relief value = constant:ireland.two } }

	# German channels: arms, intelligence, access, and emissary contact.
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_demand_proof_of_german_delivery_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_arms value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_arms value = constant:ireland.two } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_test_a_german_arms_promise_full_recorded check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_arms value = constant:ireland.three compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_arms value = constant:ireland.three } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_open_a_g2_liaison_channel_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_intelligence value = constant:ireland.three compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_intelligence value = constant:ireland.three } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_receive_a_german_emissary_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_emissary value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_emissary value = constant:ireland.two } }

	# Vatican channels: mediation, relief, access, and the public humanitarian record.
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_seek_vatican_humanitarian_mediation_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_mediation value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_mediation value = constant:ireland.two } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_conclude_a_vatican_relief_mediation_full_recorded check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_mediation value = constant:ireland.three compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_mediation value = constant:ireland.three } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_seek_vatican_humanitarian_mediation_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_relief value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_relief value = constant:ireland.two } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_seek_vatican_humanitarian_mediation_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_public value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_public value = constant:ireland.two } }

	# Neutral-state channels: consultation, shipping, access, and public forum.
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_convene_a_neutral_states_consultation_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_consultation value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_consultation value = constant:ireland.two } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_investigate_a_neutral_shipping_loss_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_shipping value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_shipping value = constant:ireland.two } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_mark_and_publicise_neutral_shipping_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_shipping value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_shipping value = constant:ireland.two } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_convene_a_neutral_states_consultation_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_public value = constant:ireland.two compare = less_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_public value = constant:ireland.two } }

	# A sustained high-exposure operational record is the explicit depth-4
	# boundary. It is never inferred from a reaction count or from a focus alone.
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_formalise_plan_w_liaison_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_foreign_exposure value = constant:ireland.cooperation_depth_4_exposure compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_intelligence value = constant:ireland.four } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_send_an_american_procurement_mission_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_foreign_exposure value = constant:ireland.cooperation_depth_4_exposure compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_procurement value = constant:ireland.four } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_demand_proof_of_german_delivery_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_foreign_exposure value = constant:ireland.cooperation_depth_4_exposure compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_arms value = constant:ireland.four } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_seek_vatican_humanitarian_mediation_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_foreign_exposure value = constant:ireland.cooperation_depth_4_exposure compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_mediation value = constant:ireland.four } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_convene_a_neutral_states_consultation_complete check_variable = { var = ireland_foreign_exposure value = constant:ireland.cooperation_depth_4_exposure compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_consultation value = constant:ireland.four } }

	# Derived scan used only by A-03 and the visible ledger audit; the twenty
	# source records above remain the authoritative history.
	set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = constant:ireland.zero }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_shipping value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_shipping } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_intelligence value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_intelligence } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_access value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_access } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_weather value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_british_weather } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_procurement value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_procurement } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_intelligence value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_intelligence } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_access value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_access } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_relief value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_american_relief } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_arms value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_arms } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_intelligence value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_intelligence } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_access value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_access } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_emissary value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_german_emissary } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_mediation value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_mediation } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_relief value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_relief } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_access value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_access } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_public value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_vatican_public } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_consultation value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_consultation } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_shipping value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_shipping } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_access value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_access } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_public value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_cooperation_depth_neutral_states_public } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_achievement_neutrality_partner_depth_max compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_neutrality_partner_depth_max value = ireland_neutrality_partner_depth_max }
	}
}

# Resolves the merged election mission without erasing the distinct general-
# election and by-election histories.
ireland_cancel_election_mission = {
	set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 4 }
	ireland_record_mission_result = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_election_mission_by_election_mode }
		set_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_cancellation_recorded
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_cancellation_recorded
		set_variable = { var = ireland_cancel_refund_code value = constant:ireland.mission_refund_code_electoral_register }
		ireland_refund_cancelled_material_inputs = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_cancelled_material_refund_tt
	}
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_active
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_partial
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_partial
	clr_country_flag = ireland_election_mission_by_election_mode
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_cleanup }
		ireland_cleanup_route_state = yes
	}
}

ireland_timeout_election_mission = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_election_mission_by_election_mode }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_partial }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_partial_success_tt
			set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 2 }
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_partial_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_dail_mandate value = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { ireland_mission_timeout_is_escalated = yes }
				custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_escalation_tt
				set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 5 }
				set_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_escalated
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_failure_tt
				set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 3 }
			}
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_failure_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_dail_mandate value = -3 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_partial }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_partial_success_tt
			set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 2 }
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_partial_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_dail_mandate value = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { ireland_mission_timeout_is_escalated = yes }
				custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_escalation_tt
				set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 5 }
				set_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_escalated
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_failure_tt
				set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 3 }
			}
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_failure_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_dail_mandate value = -3 }
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_active
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_partial
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_partial
	clr_country_flag = ireland_election_mission_by_election_mode
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_cleanup }
		ireland_cleanup_route_state = yes
	}
}

ireland_complete_election_mission = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_election_mission_by_election_mode }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_partial }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_partial_success_tt
			set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 2 }
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_partial_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_dail_mandate value = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_full_success_tt
			set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 1 }
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_full_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_dail_mandate value = 5 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_partial }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_partial_success_tt
			set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 2 }
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_partial_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_dail_mandate value = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_full_success_tt
			set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 1 }
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_full_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_dail_mandate value = 5 }
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_active
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_partial
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_by_election_partial
	clr_country_flag = ireland_election_mission_by_election_mode
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_cleanup }
		ireland_cleanup_route_state = yes
	}
}

# Resolves the two organised-society objective modes while preserving their
# separate result ledgers.
ireland_cancel_service_obligations_mission = {
	set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 4 }
	ireland_record_mission_result = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_service_mission_parish_audit_mode }
		set_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_cancellation_recorded
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_cancellation_recorded
	}
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_active
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_partial
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_partial
	clr_country_flag = ireland_service_mission_parish_audit_mode
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_cleanup }
		ireland_cleanup_route_state = yes
	}
}

ireland_timeout_service_obligations_mission = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_service_mission_parish_audit_mode }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_partial }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_partial_success_tt
			set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 2 }
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_partial_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_administrative_reach value = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { ireland_mission_timeout_is_escalated = yes }
				custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_escalation_tt
				set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 5 }
				set_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_escalated
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_failure_tt
				set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 3 }
			}
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_failure_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_administrative_reach value = -3 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_partial }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_partial_success_tt
			set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 2 }
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_partial_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_administrative_reach value = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { ireland_mission_timeout_is_escalated = yes }
				custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_escalation_tt
				set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 5 }
				set_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_escalated
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_failure_tt
				set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 3 }
			}
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_failure_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_administrative_reach value = -3 }
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_active
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_partial
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_partial
	clr_country_flag = ireland_service_mission_parish_audit_mode
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_cleanup }
		ireland_cleanup_route_state = yes
	}
}

ireland_complete_service_obligations_mission = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_service_mission_parish_audit_mode }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_partial }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_partial_success_tt
			set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 2 }
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_partial_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_administrative_reach value = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_full_success_tt
			set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 1 }
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_full_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_administrative_reach value = 5 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_partial }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_partial_success_tt
			set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 2 }
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_partial_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_administrative_reach value = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ireland_mission_full_success_tt
			set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = 1 }
			ireland_record_mission_result = yes
			set_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_full_recorded
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_administrative_reach value = 5 }
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_active
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_partial
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_keep_essential_services_running_parish_audit_partial
	clr_country_flag = ireland_service_mission_parish_audit_mode
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_cleanup }
		ireland_cleanup_route_state = yes
	}
}

ireland_adjust_radical_org_progress = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_radical_org_ira_mode }
		add_to_variable = { var = ireland_republican_network_strength value = ireland_radical_progress_delta }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_radical_org_congress_mode }
		add_to_variable = { var = ireland_congress_coalition_cohesion value = ireland_radical_progress_delta }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_radical_org_corporate_mode }
		add_to_variable = { var = ireland_corporate_network_strength value = ireland_radical_progress_delta }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_radical_org_ailtiri_mode }
		add_to_variable = { var = ireland_ailtiri_cell_strength value = ireland_radical_progress_delta }
	}
	set_variable = { var = ireland_radical_progress_delta value = constant:ireland.zero }
}

ireland_adjust_radical_constituent_progress = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_radical_constituent_ira_mode }

		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_radical_constituent_congress_mode }
		add_to_variable = { var = ireland_social_consent value = ireland_radical_progress_delta }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_radical_constituent_corporate_mode }
		add_to_variable = { var = ireland_corporate_network_strength value = ireland_radical_progress_delta }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_radical_constituent_ailtiri_mode }
		add_to_variable = { var = ireland_national_settlement value = ireland_radical_progress_delta }
	}
	set_variable = { var = ireland_radical_progress_delta value = constant:ireland.zero }
}

ireland_sync_foreign_channels = {
	# Partner defeat closes the channel and records an actionable dependency
	# failure. Leverage and reaction history are deliberately retained for the
	# foreign and postwar reviews; only the invalid route is removed.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_british_channel_open
			NOT = { ireland_british_partner_valid = yes }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ireland_british_channel_open
		set_country_flag = ireland_foreign_british_channel_invalidated
		set_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_american_channel_open
			NOT = { ireland_american_partner_valid = yes }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ireland_american_channel_open
		set_country_flag = ireland_foreign_american_channel_invalidated
		set_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_german_channel_open
			NOT = { ireland_german_partner_valid = yes }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ireland_german_channel_open
		set_country_flag = ireland_foreign_german_channel_invalidated
		set_country_flag = ireland_foreign_sponsor_collapse
		set_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_vatican_channel_open
			NOT = { ireland_vatican_partner_valid = yes }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ireland_vatican_channel_open
		set_country_flag = ireland_foreign_vatican_channel_invalidated
		set_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure
	}
}

	set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_cap_cultural_archival value = constant:ireland.mission_category_cap_routine }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_cap_radical_underground value = constant:ireland.mission_category_cap_routine }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_cap_provision value = constant:ireland.mission_category_cap_provision }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_cap_readiness value = constant:ireland.mission_category_cap_readiness }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_cap_neutrality_incidents value = constant:ireland.mission_category_cap_neutrality }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_cap_foreign value = constant:ireland.mission_category_cap_routine }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_cap_northern value = constant:ireland.mission_category_cap_northern }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_cap_occupation_integration value = constant:ireland.mission_category_cap_occupation }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_cap_regional_external value = constant:ireland.mission_category_cap_routine }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_mission_count_settlement_government value = ireland_mission_cap_settlement_government compare = greater_than_or_equals } }

	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_category_postwar_at_capacity }

	# A critical overlay reserves one global slot. Critical mission blocks are
	# explicitly exempted in the decision file; routine missions must wait.
	clr_country_flag = ireland_critical_component_slot_needed
	clr_country_flag = ireland_invasion_slot_needed
	clr_country_flag = ireland_neutrality_incident_slot_needed
	clr_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_slot_needed
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_slot_needed
	clr_country_flag = ireland_occupation_slot_needed
	clr_country_flag = ireland_route_review_slot_needed
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_provision_severe_bottleneck has_country_flag = ireland_provision_dual_bottleneck has_country_flag = ireland_provision_zero_component has_country_flag = ireland_national_emergency_active has_war = yes } }
		set_country_flag = ireland_critical_component_slot_needed
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_invasion_danger has_country_flag = ireland_invasion_danger has_war = yes } }
		set_country_flag = ireland_invasion_slot_needed
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_neutrality_incident_active }
		set_country_flag = ireland_neutrality_incident_slot_needed
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_election_calendar_ready has_country_flag = ireland_government_formation_ready has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_constitutional_crisis has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_crisis_active } }
		set_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_slot_needed
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_active has_country_flag = ireland_northern_plebiscite_open has_country_flag = ireland_northern_integration_active has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_northern_occupation_and_integration } }
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_slot_needed
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_active has_country_flag = ireland_northern_integration_active has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_northern_occupation_and_integration } }
		set_country_flag = ireland_occupation_slot_needed
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_route_conclusion_review_and_consolidation has_country_flag = ireland_route_review_active has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_active } }
		set_country_flag = ireland_route_review_slot_needed
	}
	set_variable = { var = ireland_critical_slots_required value = constant:ireland.mission_reserved_slots_normal }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_national_emergency_active
				has_war = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_critical_slots_required value = constant:ireland.mission_reserved_slots_emergency }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_national_emergency_active
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_active
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_critical_slots_required value = constant:ireland.mission_reserved_slots_emergency_occupation }
	}
	set_variable = { var = ireland_critical_reservation_threshold value = ireland_mission_capacity }
	subtract_from_variable = { ireland_critical_reservation_threshold = ireland_critical_slots_required }
	clamp_variable = { var = ireland_critical_reservation_threshold min = constant:ireland.zero max = constant:ireland.mission_cap_emergency_occupation }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ireland_active_mission_count value = ireland_critical_reservation_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_critical_component_slot_needed
				has_country_flag = ireland_invasion_slot_needed
				has_country_flag = ireland_neutrality_incident_slot_needed
				has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_slot_needed
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_slot_needed
				has_country_flag = ireland_occupation_slot_needed
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_review_slot_needed
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_critical_slot_reserved
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = ireland_critical_slot_reserved
	}
}

# The vanilla mission API exposes remove_mission and add_days_mission_timeout,
# but no pause/resume effect. These helpers preserve the engine timeout before
# removing a displaced routine mission, then restore that exact remaining time
# when the reserved slot is released. A paused mission never records a result.
ireland_preempt_lowest_priority_routine_mission_preserving_timeout = {
	clr_country_flag = ireland_preemption_taken
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_preemption_taken }
			has_country_flag = ireland_mission_verify_the_restoration_evidence_active

		clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_verify_the_restoration_evidence_complete
		clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_verify_the_restoration_evidence_partial

ireland_admit_critical_mission = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			# Critical activation is admitted before the counter rebuild. A
			# full-cap admission therefore rebuilds as capacity + 1; strict
			# greater-than preserves a free reserved slot when the critical
			# mission entered below the cap.
			check_variable = { var = ireland_active_mission_count value = ireland_mission_capacity compare = greater_than }
			ireland_has_active_critical_mission = yes
			ireland_has_preemptible_routine_mission = yes
		}
		ireland_preempt_lowest_priority_routine_mission_preserving_timeout = yes
		ireland_rebuild_mission_counters = yes
	}
}

ireland_resume_paused_routine_mission_preserving_timeout = {
	clr_country_flag = ireland_resume_taken
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_cleanup
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_archived
			}
		}
		# Route archival is terminal for transient work. Mark the one-shot
		# guard so no paused mission can be resurrected by the daily hook.
		set_country_flag = ireland_resume_taken
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_resume_taken }
			ireland_has_active_mission_capacity = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_critical_slot_reserved }
			has_country_flag = ireland_mission_verify_the_restoration_evidence_paused
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_mission_verify_the_restoration_evidence_active
		activate_mission = ireland_mission_verify_the_restoration_evidence
		set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = days_mission_timeout@ireland_mission_verify_the_restoration_evidence }
		multiply_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = -1 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = ireland_mission_verify_the_restoration_evidence_paused_remaining_days }
		add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = ireland_mission_verify_the_restoration_evidence days = ireland_mission_resume_delta }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_verify_the_restoration_evidence_paused_remaining_days value = constant:ireland.zero }
		clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_verify_the_restoration_evidence_paused
		set_country_flag = ireland_resume_taken
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_resume_taken }
			ireland_has_active_mission_capacity = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_critical_slot_reserved }
			has_country_flag = ireland_mission_collect_a_national_folklore_cycle_paused
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_mission_collect_a_national_folklore_cycle_active
		activate_mission = ireland_mission_collect_a_national_folklore_cycle
		set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = days_mission_timeout@ireland_mission_collect_a_national_folklore_cycle }
		multiply_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = -1 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = ireland_mission_collect_a_national_folklore_cycle_paused_remaining_days }
		add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = ireland_mission_collect_a_national_folklore_cycle days = ireland_mission_resume_delta }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_collect_a_national_folklore_cycle_paused_remaining_days value = constant:ireland.zero }
		clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_collect_a_national_folklore_cycle_paused
		set_country_flag = ireland_resume_taken
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_resume_taken }
			ireland_has_active_mission_capacity = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_critical_slot_reserved }
			has_country_flag = ireland_mission_hold_a_five_region_cultural_congress_paused
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_mission_hold_a_five_region_cultural_congress_active
		activate_mission = ireland_mission_hold_a_five_region_cultural_congress
		set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = days_mission_timeout@ireland_mission_hold_a_five_region_cultural_congress }
		multiply_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = -1 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = ireland_mission_hold_a_five_region_cultural_congress_paused_remaining_days }
		add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = ireland_mission_hold_a_five_region_cultural_congress days = ireland_mission_resume_delta }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_hold_a_five_region_cultural_congress_paused_remaining_days value = constant:ireland.zero }
		clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_hold_a_five_region_cultural_congress_paused
		set_country_flag = ireland_resume_taken
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_resume_taken }
			ireland_has_active_mission_capacity = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_critical_slot_reserved }
			has_country_flag = ireland_mission_consolidate_the_movement_network_paused
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_mission_consolidate_the_movement_network_active
		activate_mission = ireland_mission_consolidate_the_movement_network
		set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = days_mission_timeout@ireland_mission_consolidate_the_movement_network }
		multiply_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = -1 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = ireland_mission_consolidate_the_movement_network_paused_remaining_days }
		add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = ireland_mission_consolidate_the_movement_network days = ireland_mission_resume_delta }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_consolidate_the_movement_network_paused_remaining_days value = constant:ireland.zero }
		clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_consolidate_the_movement_network_paused
		set_country_flag = ireland_resume_taken
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_resume_taken }
			ireland_has_active_mission_capacity = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_critical_slot_reserved }
			has_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_paused
			has_country_flag = ireland_election_mission_by_election_mode
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_active
		activate_mission = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election
		set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = days_mission_timeout@ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election }
		multiply_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = -1 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_paused_remaining_days }
		add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election days = ireland_mission_resume_delta }

		clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_paused

		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_mission_assemble_seventy_routine_votes_active
		activate_mission = ireland_mission_assemble_seventy_routine_votes

		add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = ireland_mission_assemble_seventy_routine_votes days = ireland_mission_resume_delta }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_assemble_seventy_routine_votes_paused_remaining_days value = constant:ireland.zero }
		clr_country_flag = ireland_mission_assemble_seventy_routine_votes_paused
		set_country_flag = ireland_resume_taken
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_resume_taken }
			ireland_has_active_mission_capacity = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_critical_slot_reserved }
			has_country_flag = ireland_mission_maintain_cabinet_discipline_paused
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_mission_maintain_cabinet_discipline_active
		activate_mission = ireland_mission_maintain_cabinet_discipline
		set_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = days_mission_timeout@ireland_mission_maintain_cabinet_discipline }
		multiply_variable = { var = ireland_mission_resume_delta value = -1 }

ireland_sync_hidden_permissions = {
	clr_country_flag = ireland_hidden_content_permission
	clr_country_flag = ireland_hidden_content_game_rule_enabled
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_game_rule = {
				rule = ireland_hidden_content
				option = ENABLED
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_hidden_content_game_rule_enabled
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_hidden_permission
			has_country_flag = ireland_hidden_content_game_rule_enabled
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_hidden_content_permission
	}
	# The reveal is a five-layer stack: serious public capacity, independent evidence,
	# a named political opening, and an unusual campaign condition. These flags are
	# derived from completed work; none is a random or ideology-only shortcut.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_serious_cultural_stewardship_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_site_stewardship_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_archival_record_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_cultural_evidence_ledger_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_cultural_language_service value = constant:ireland.cultural_high_king_language compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_cultural_archival_record value = constant:ireland.cultural_high_king_archival compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_cultural_regional_participation value = constant:ireland.cultural_high_king_regional compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_cultural_site_stewardship value = constant:ireland.cultural_high_king_site compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_concealed_evidence_credible_count value = constant:ireland.cultural_evidence_royal_credible compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_concealed_evidence_category_count value = constant:ireland.cultural_evidence_royal_categories compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_concealed_evidence_region_count value = constant:ireland.cultural_evidence_royal_regions compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = ireland_concealed_evidence_disqualifier_count value = constant:ireland.zero compare = greater_than } }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_high_king_evidence_verified
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_high_king_evidence_verified
			has_country_flag = ireland_regional_participation_complete
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_draft_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_stewardship_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_dail_supremacy_clause_complete
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_high_king_convention_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_high_king_convention_ready
			OR = {
				has_war = yes
				has_country_flag = ireland_neutrality_incident_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_window_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure
				has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_review_started
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_high_king_rare_condition
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_regional_participation_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_ledger_open
			has_country_flag = ireland_site_stewardship_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_archival_record_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_cultural_archival_record value = constant:ireland.cultural_five_provinces_archival compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_cultural_regional_participation value = constant:ireland.cultural_five_provinces_regional compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_cultural_site_stewardship value = constant:ireland.cultural_five_provinces_site compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = ireland_concealed_evidence_disqualifier_count value = constant:ireland.zero compare = greater_than } }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_mandates_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_mandates_ready
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_federal_preparation_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_action_convene_a_federal_powers_conference_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_regional_review_complete
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_convention_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_convention_ready
			OR = {
				has_war = yes
				has_country_flag = ireland_neutrality_incident_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_window_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_regional_review_complete
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_rare_condition
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_hidden_content_permission
			has_country_flag = ireland_hidden_content_game_rule_enabled
			has_country_flag = ireland_route_five_provinces
			has_country_flag = ireland_federal_convention_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_rare_condition
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_event_seen_217 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_impossible_disqualified }
		}
		country_event = { id = ireland_events.217 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_hidden_content_permission
			has_country_flag = ireland_serious_cultural_stewardship_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_site_stewardship_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_cultural_evidence_ledger_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_cultural_language_service value = constant:ireland.cultural_otherworld_language compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_cultural_archival_record value = constant:ireland.cultural_otherworld_archival compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_cultural_regional_participation value = constant:ireland.cultural_otherworld_regional compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_cultural_site_stewardship value = constant:ireland.cultural_otherworld_site compare = greater_than_or_equals }

	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_investigation_ready
			check_variable = { var = ireland_concealed_evidence_credible_count value = constant:ireland.cultural_evidence_otherworld_credible compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_concealed_evidence_category_count value = constant:ireland.cultural_evidence_otherworld_categories compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_concealed_evidence_region_count value = constant:ireland.cultural_evidence_otherworld_regions compare = greater_than_or_equals }

				has_country_flag = ireland_neutrality_incident_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_window_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_action_convene_a_hidden_evidence_council_complete
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_rare_condition
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_investigation_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_evidence_verified
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_independent_witnesses_ready
	}
	# The fourth reveal is an explicit player-facing event. It is admitted only
	# after the investigation, independent witnesses, evidence ledger, unusual
	# campaign condition, and the named restoration/council authority all exist.
	# The event itself, rather than this refresh pass, writes the impossible and
	# verified-impossibility flags.
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = IRE
			has_country_flag = ireland_initialized
			has_country_flag = ireland_hidden_content_permission
			has_country_flag = ireland_hidden_content_game_rule_enabled
			has_country_flag = ireland_route_otherworld
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_investigation_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_evidence_verified
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_witnesses_verified
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_evidence_ledger_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_verified
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_rare_condition
			has_country_flag = ireland_action_convene_a_hidden_evidence_council_complete
			ireland_concealed_transition_authority = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_event_seen_437 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_archive_reveal_complete }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_closed }
			date > 1937.1.1
			date < 1956.1.1
		}
		country_event = { id = ireland_events.437 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_high_king_convention_complete
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_draft_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_dail_supremacy_clause_complete
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_high_king_constitutional_mandate
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_high_king_convention_complete
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_elective_crown_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_guardian_crown_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_high_king_royal_household_path
				has_country_flag = ireland_high_king_sacral_path
				has_country_flag = ireland_high_king_captured_crown_path
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_high_king_ratification_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_federal_convention_complete
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_action_convene_a_federal_powers_conference_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_federal_preparation_complete
			}
		}

	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_federal_convention_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_policy_proof_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ulster_rule_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_archive_reveal_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_compact_ready
		set_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_containment_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_compact_with_otherworld_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_containment_protocol_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_verified
			has_country_flag = ireland_action_convene_a_hidden_evidence_council_complete
			ireland_concealed_transition_authority = yes
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_impossible_confirmed
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_guardian_order_ready
		set_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_dominion_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_guardians_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_policy_proof_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_regional_thresholds_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_hidden_content_permission
			has_country_flag = ireland_action_convene_a_hidden_evidence_council_complete
			ireland_concealed_transition_authority = yes
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_high_king_impossible_confirmed
				has_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_impossible_confirmed
			}
		}
		# This shared flag belongs to the High-King/Five-Provinces reveal layer.
		# Otherworld uses its route-specific impossible-confirmed flag below so
		# this writer cannot suppress or bypass event 437.
		set_country_flag = ireland_verified_impossible_reveal
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_high_king_convention_complete }
		set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_reveal_royal_convention
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_federal_convention_complete }
		set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_reveal_fivefold_convention
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_evidence_ledger_complete }
		set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_reveal_otherworld_evidence_review
		set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_reveal_sacral_or_otherworld
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_compact_with_otherworld_complete }
		set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_reveal_two_irelands_compact
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_fivefold_obligation_complete }
		set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_reveal_sacral_or_otherworld
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_june_1942_public_launch_complete }
		set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_reveal_ailtiri_public_launch
	}
}

ireland_enforce_hpa_boundary = {
	# H, P, and A are mutually exclusive runtime classifications. The
	# classification is derived from the route profile and never from ideology.
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_historical }
		clr_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_plausible
		clr_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_absurd
		clr_country_flag = ireland_plausible_route_active
		clr_country_flag = ireland_absurd_route_active
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_absurd }
		clr_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_historical
		clr_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_plausible
		clr_country_flag = ireland_plausible_route_active
		set_country_flag = ireland_absurd_route_active
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_plausible }
		clr_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_historical
		clr_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_absurd
		clr_country_flag = ireland_absurd_route_active
		set_country_flag = ireland_plausible_route_active
	}
}

	clr_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_absurd
	clr_country_flag = ireland_plausible_route_active
	clr_country_flag = ireland_absurd_route_active
	# One ordered selector owns the durable profile. Route defaults are lower
	# priority than exact subprofiles, and the convergence merger is not active
	# until its 180-day conclusion review succeeds.
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_convergence_order_concluded }
		set_country_flag = ireland_profile_convergence_order
		set_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_absurd
		set_country_flag = ireland_absurd_route_active
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_convergence_order_capstone_complete }
		# The balanced compact is the host during the full convergence review.
		# Component route flags remain historical evidence, not simultaneous
		# durable profiles.
		set_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_compact
		set_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_absurd
		set_country_flag = ireland_absurd_route_active
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_route_otherworld
			has_country_flag = ireland_dominion_claim_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_impossible_confirmed

		set_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_dominion
		set_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_absurd
		set_country_flag = ireland_absurd_route_active
	}
	else_if = {

			has_country_flag = ireland_route_otherworld
			has_country_flag = ireland_containment_protocol_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_containment

		# independently verified impossible result is accepted. The same
		# durable profile therefore crosses P into A without changing family.

			set_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_absurd
			set_country_flag = ireland_absurd_route_active
		}

			set_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_plausible
			set_country_flag = ireland_plausible_route_active
		}
	}

	else = {
		set_country_flag = ireland_profile_historical_de_valera
		set_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_historical
	}
	ireland_enforce_hpa_boundary = yes
	clr_country_flag = ireland_route_profile_sync_required
}

ireland_sync_route_gate_flags = {
	# Clann is a support profile: its route conclusion is a host-government
	# qualification, not an automatic constitutional transformation. The
	# qualification only opens after the agrarian programme and the shared
	# material proof are both recorded.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_route_clann_agrarian_broker
			has_country_flag = ireland_rural_producer_compact_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_material_conclusion_proof_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_clann_host_conclusion_ready
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = ireland_clann_host_conclusion_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_clann_founding_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_rural_price_board_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_agrarian_cohesion value = constant:ireland.agrarian_bloc_cohesion_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_agrarian_bloc_ready
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = ireland_agrarian_bloc_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_draft_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_church_labour_consultation_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_minority_guarantees_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_social_consent value = constant:ireland.vocational_mandate_consent_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_minority_confidence value = constant:ireland.vocational_mandate_minority_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_vocational_mandate_ready
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = ireland_vocational_mandate_ready
	}
}

ireland_advance_dillon_mandate = {
	# A documented public intervention choice advances the live mandate in
	# proportion to the consent that can sustain it. This is a route ledger
	# change, not a passive political-power income stream.
	set_temp_variable = { ireland_dillon_mandate_delta = ireland_social_consent }
	divide_temp_variable = { ireland_dillon_mandate_delta = constant:ireland.value_max }
	multiply_temp_variable = { ireland_dillon_mandate_delta = constant:ireland.dillon_mandate_flavour_gain }
	add_to_variable = { ireland_intervention_mandate = ireland_dillon_mandate_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = ireland_intervention_mandate min = constant:ireland.value_min max = constant:ireland.value_max }
}

ireland_setback_dillon_mandate = {
	# Setbacks are exposure-sensitive: both compromised neutrality and British
	# pressure make the same public argument harder to recover.
	set_temp_variable = { ireland_dillon_mandate_delta = ireland_neutrality_integrity }
	divide_temp_variable = { ireland_dillon_mandate_delta = constant:ireland.value_max }
	multiply_temp_variable = { ireland_dillon_mandate_delta = constant:ireland.dillon_mandate_flavour_loss }
	multiply_temp_variable = { ireland_dillon_mandate_delta = -1 }
	add_to_variable = { ireland_intervention_mandate = ireland_dillon_mandate_delta }
	set_temp_variable = { ireland_dillon_mandate_delta = ireland_british_leverage }
	divide_temp_variable = { ireland_dillon_mandate_delta = constant:ireland.value_max }
	multiply_temp_variable = { ireland_dillon_mandate_delta = constant:ireland.dillon_mandate_flavour_loss }
	multiply_temp_variable = { ireland_dillon_mandate_delta = -1 }
	add_to_variable = { ireland_intervention_mandate = ireland_dillon_mandate_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = ireland_intervention_mandate min = constant:ireland.value_min max = constant:ireland.value_max }
}

ireland_sync_dillon_route_state = {
	# A sovereignty mandate, foreign safeguard record, or named charter is the
	# legal base. The detailed Dillon safeguards are live projections of separate
	# material, diplomatic, law, and Northern records below.
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_sovereignty_mandate_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_safeguards_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_action_draft_a_unitary_safeguards_charter_complete
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_sovereignty_safeguards_active
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = ireland_sovereignty_safeguards_active
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_route_dillon_intervention
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_closed }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_equip_a_reserve_brigade_full_recorded }
			set_country_flag = ireland_home_defence_reserve_protected
		}
		else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_home_defence_reserve_protected }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_meet_the_member_contribution_schedule_full_recorded }
			set_country_flag = ireland_military_contribution_defined
		}
		else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_military_contribution_defined }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_mission_prove_the_command_district_full_recorded
					has_country_flag = ireland_action_integrate_or_devolve_military_command_complete
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_command_safeguard_enforceable
		}
		else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_command_safeguard_enforceable }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_action_answer_a_foreign_access_request_complete
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_safeguards_complete
						has_country_flag = ireland_law_foreign_access_active
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_access_safeguard_enforceable
		}
		else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_access_safeguard_enforceable }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_action_draft_a_unitary_safeguards_charter_complete
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_safeguards_complete
						has_country_flag = ireland_law_trade_shipping_active
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_customs_safeguard_enforceable
		}
		else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_customs_safeguard_enforceable }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_action_merge_tax_and_debt_administration_complete
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_safeguards_complete
						has_country_flag = ireland_law_guarantee_active
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_finance_safeguard_enforceable
		}
		else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_finance_safeguard_enforceable }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_action_prepare_a_wartime_unity_bargain_complete
					has_country_flag = ireland_northern_wartime_bargain_complete
					has_country_flag = ireland_northern_deferment_accepted
					has_country_flag = ireland_northern_policy_active
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_northern_safeguard_enforceable
		}
		else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_safeguard_enforceable }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_action_review_foreign_guarantees_complete
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_safeguards_complete
						has_country_flag = ireland_law_guarantee_active
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_withdrawal_safeguard_enforceable
			set_country_flag = ireland_review_safeguard_enforceable
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = ireland_withdrawal_safeguard_enforceable
			clr_country_flag = ireland_review_safeguard_enforceable
		}
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = ireland_home_defence_reserve_protected
		clr_country_flag = ireland_military_contribution_defined
		clr_country_flag = ireland_access_safeguard_enforceable
		clr_country_flag = ireland_command_safeguard_enforceable
		clr_country_flag = ireland_customs_safeguard_enforceable
		clr_country_flag = ireland_finance_safeguard_enforceable
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_safeguard_enforceable
		clr_country_flag = ireland_withdrawal_safeguard_enforceable
		clr_country_flag = ireland_review_safeguard_enforceable
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_route_dillon_intervention
			has_country_flag = ireland_dillon_public_dissent_complete
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_closed }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_intervention_mandate value = constant:ireland.dillon_caucus_mandate compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_dillon_mandate_complete
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = ireland_dillon_mandate_complete
	}
	# A caucus and a political gate only make a named Dáil vote admissible.
	# The assent flag is written by the explicit P04 vote event after the
	# confidence mission hands the question to the chamber.
	if = {
		limit = { ireland_has_dillon_safeguards = yes }
		set_country_flag = ireland_dillon_safeguards_ready
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = ireland_dillon_safeguards_ready
	}
}

ireland_sync_political_gate_flags = {
	# Keep the visible Dáil/Seanad panel synchronized with all daily political
	# effects, including actions that were authored before the chamber ledger.
	ireland_update_parliament = yes
	# Election and constitutional handoffs are durable only after the election
	# mission has recorded a full or partial result. Opening seat variables alone
	# never create a mandate flag.
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_full_recorded
				has_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_free_general_election_partial_recorded
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_election_result_ready
	}
	if = {

		}

	}
	if = {

			has_country_flag = ireland_election_result_ready

				has_country_flag = ireland_government_formation_complete

			check_variable = { var = ireland_administrative_reach value = constant:ireland.political_gate_admin_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }

			check_variable = { var = ireland_social_consent value = constant:ireland.political_gate_social_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }

		set_country_flag = ireland_party_conference_ready
	}
	if = {

			has_country_flag = ireland_party_conference_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_election_mandate_complete

			check_variable = { var = ireland_labour_organisation value = constant:ireland.constitutional_route_labour_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}

	}
	if = {

			has_country_flag = ireland_state_we_built_complete

		}

	if = {

		}

		set_country_flag = ireland_armed_neutrality_mandate

	if = {

			has_country_flag = ireland_command_audit_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_security_loyalty value = constant:ireland.political_gate_security_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}

		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_accounting_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_conclusion_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_demobilisation_ready
	}

	# Current crisis signals are derived from live failure conditions. They are
	# not route victories and are cleared when the underlying condition recovers.
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = ireland_security_loyalty value = constant:ireland.republican_route_security_max compare = less_than_or_equals }
				has_country_flag = ireland_military_command_collapse
				has_country_flag = ireland_security_command_collapse
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_security_crisis
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = ireland_security_crisis
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_overreach
				has_country_flag = ireland_security_crisis
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_government_formation_complete
					check_variable = { var = ireland_dail_government_formal_seats value = ireland_dail_majority_threshold compare = less_than }

			}
		}

	else = {
