	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_overreach
				has_country_flag = ireland_military_command_collapse
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_route_committed
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure_recovered }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = ireland_missions_failed value = constant:ireland.zero compare = greater_than }
						check_variable = { var = ireland_missions_escalated value = constant:ireland.zero compare = greater_than }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_route_failure
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_security_crisis
				has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_crisis_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure
				check_variable = { var = ireland_national_settlement value = constant:ireland.settlement_stage_3_min compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_radical_transition_gate
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = ireland_radical_transition_gate
	}

	# Republican and workers' access uses the live crisis plus named public work.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_public_order_review_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_republican_veterans_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_republican_network_strength value = constant:ireland.republican_route_network_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_security_crisis
				has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_crisis_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure
				check_variable = { var = ireland_national_settlement value = constant:ireland.settlement_stage_3_min compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ira_organization_ready
		set_country_flag = ireland_ira_public_base_ready
		set_country_flag = ireland_security_exposure_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_ira_organization_ready
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_security_crisis
				check_variable = { var = ireland_national_settlement value = constant:ireland.settlement_stage_3_min compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
			check_variable = { var = ireland_security_loyalty value = constant:ireland.republican_route_security_max compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ira_transition_ready
		set_country_flag = ireland_ira_state_interface_broken
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_church_labour_consultation_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_labour_organisation value = constant:ireland.labour_route_decisive_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_republican_network_strength value = constant:ireland.republican_route_network_negotiated_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = ireland_social_consent value = constant:ireland.labour_route_distress_max compare = less_than_or_equals }
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure
				has_country_flag = ireland_congress_crisis
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_ira_republican }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_labour_organisation_decisive
		set_country_flag = ireland_congress_organization_ready
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = ireland_labour_organisation_decisive
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_congress_organization_ready
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = ireland_social_consent value = constant:ireland.labour_route_distress_max compare = less_than_or_equals }
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_congress_transition_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_congress_reform_bridge_complete }
		set_country_flag = ireland_union_local_mandate_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_union_local_mandate_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_union_commitment value = constant:ireland.constitutional_route_labour_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_movement_unity value = constant:ireland.labour_route_social_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_congress_plural_mandate_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_union_local_mandate_complete
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = ireland_movement_unity value = constant:ireland.labour_route_distress_max compare = less_than }
				has_country_flag = ireland_security_crisis
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_congress_vanguard_capture_ready
	}

	# O'Duffy's P route needs a named residue, crisis pressure, and domestic
	# organisation before the player can close the chapter and reopen it.
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_security_crisis
				has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_crisis_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure
			}
			check_variable = { var = ireland_social_consent value = constant:ireland.oduffy_route_social_max compare = less_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_rural_organisation value = constant:ireland.oduffy_route_network_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_agrarian_cohesion value = constant:ireland.oduffy_route_network_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_fine_gael_mandate
				has_country_flag = ireland_government_formation_complete
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_oduffy_crisis_ready
		set_country_flag = ireland_oduffy_network_ready
		set_country_flag = ireland_oduffy_state_interface_broken
		set_country_flag = ireland_oduffy_chapter_open
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_oduffy_network_revival_complete
			date < 1944.11.30
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_oduffy_personal_command_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_route_oduffy
			has_country_flag = ireland_veteran_employer_league_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_producer_accounting_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_corporate_estates_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_corporate_estates_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_army_acceptance value = constant:ireland.political_gate_security_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_oduffy_army_acceptance_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_army_acceptance_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_corporate_estates_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_coalition_cohesion value = constant:ireland.constitutional_route_coalition_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_corporate_order_ready
	}

	# Clerical and producer-state access comes from an authored vocational failure
	# and three live institutional centres, not from Church influence alone.
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_vocational_failure_bridge_ready
				has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_overreach
				has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_crisis_active
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = ireland_dail_mandate value = constant:ireland.clerical_route_dail_max compare = less_than_or_equals }
				has_country_flag = ireland_vocational_failure_bridge_ready
			}
			check_variable = { var = ireland_church_institutional_influence value = constant:ireland.clerical_route_council_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_administrative_reach value = constant:ireland.clerical_route_admin_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_rural_organisation value = constant:ireland.clann_route_rural_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_labour_organisation value = constant:ireland.constitutional_route_labour_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_clerical_failure_ready
		set_country_flag = ireland_service_state_ready
		set_country_flag = ireland_producer_mandate_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_clerical_failure_bridge_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_producer_accounting_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_producer_councils_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_service_state_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_church_institutional_influence value = constant:ireland.clerical_route_council_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_minority_confidence value = constant:ireland.constitutional_route_minority_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_clerical_guardianship_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_producer_councils_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_clerical_guardianship_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_producer_constitution_ready
	}

	# The dated Ailtirí gate remains hidden until culture, crisis, and date all
	# agree. Public launch is a separate state from the later state capture.
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1940.9.1
			has_country_flag = ireland_serious_cultural_stewardship_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_cultural_evidence_ledger_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_cultural_language_service value = constant:ireland.ailtiri_route_cultural_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_cultural_archival_record value = constant:ireland.ailtiri_route_cultural_min compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_national_emergency_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_overreach
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ailtiri_cells_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1942.6.1
			has_country_flag = ireland_ailtiri_cells_ready
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_overreach
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure
				has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_crisis_active
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ailtiri_public_launch_ready
		set_country_flag = ireland_ailtiri_crisis
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_ailtiri_public_launch
			check_variable = { var = ireland_administrative_reach value = constant:ireland.ailtiri_route_admin_max compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ailtiri_state_capture_ready
		set_country_flag = ireland_ailtiri_leadership_ready
	}

	# Explicitly accepted impossibility follows the named convention, not the
	# initial hidden permission. This is the H/P-to-A boundary for the cultural
	# conclusions.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_hidden_content_permission
			has_country_flag = ireland_hidden_content_game_rule_enabled
			has_country_flag = ireland_action_convene_a_hidden_evidence_council_complete
			ireland_concealed_transition_authority = yes
			has_country_flag = ireland_federal_convention_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_rare_condition
			has_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_stone_of_divisions_verified
			has_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_standards_accepted
			has_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_sites_secured
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_impossible_disqualified }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_impossible_confirmed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_federal_republic_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_pentarchy_guardians_complete
			}
			has_country_flag = ireland_ulster_rule_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_mandate_conclusion_valid
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_crown_province_convergence_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_integration_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_provision_conclusion_ready
			has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_conclusion_ready
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_convergence_material_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_fivefold_obligation_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_postwar_review_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_convergence_foreign_ready
	}

	# Late H/P handoffs are authored by the named work that makes each project
	# credible. Threshold-dependent flags are refreshed daily so the Dillon and
	# security gates cannot outlive the live ledger that justified them.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_sovereignty_mandate_complete
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_british_leverage
				value = constant:ireland.dillon_british_leverage_max
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_british_leverage_below_intervention_limit
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = ireland_british_leverage_below_intervention_limit
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_republican_legitimacy_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_republican_veterans_complete
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_administrative_reach
				value = constant:ireland.political_gate_admin_min
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_security_loyalty
				value = constant:ireland.political_gate_security_min
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_reintegration_capacity_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_public_order_review_complete
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_security_loyalty
				value = constant:ireland.political_gate_security_min
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_security_review_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_baseline_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_public_order_review_complete
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_security_loyalty
				value = constant:ireland.political_gate_security_min
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_territorial_defence_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_treaty_port_transfer_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_port_defence_authority_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_strict_neutrality_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_treaty_port_transfer_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_port_diplomacy_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_command_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_civil_service_and_courts_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_professional_command_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_development_authority_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_social_cooperatives_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_producer_accounting_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_dail_supremacy_clause_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_civil_service_and_courts_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_church_labour_consultation_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_minority_guarantees_complete
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_social_consent
				value = constant:ireland.political_gate_social_min
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_vocational_domains_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_convention_of_commands_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_ledger_open
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ira_command_ready
		set_country_flag = ireland_ira_local_mandates_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_republic_in_districts_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_ledger_open
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ira_civil_authority_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_arms_before_authority_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_safeguards_complete
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ira_foreign_channel_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_authority_before_arms_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_ledger_open
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ira_northern_command_ready
	}
}

ireland_refresh_route_spirits = {
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_spirit_sync_required has_country_flag = ireland_route_committed } }
		# Three persistent families are kept mutually exclusive: governing order, organised society, and security/external position.
		# A route installs one trio; a failure flag removes and replaces only its affected family slot.
		ireland_remove_governing_spirit_forms = yes
		ireland_remove_society_spirit_forms = yes
		ireland_remove_security_spirit_forms = yes
		# Durable profile flags are exclusive. This selector therefore installs
		# exactly one trio, with the opening trio retained for support/recovery
		# profiles that have no independent sovereign conclusion.
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_convergence_order }
			add_ideas = {
				ireland_spirit_governing_convergence_order
				ireland_spirit_society_convergence_order
				ireland_spirit_security_convergence_order
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_dominion }
			add_ideas = {
				ireland_spirit_governing_otherworld_dominion
				ireland_spirit_society_otherworld_dominion
				ireland_spirit_security_otherworld_dominion
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_containment }
			add_ideas = {
				ireland_spirit_governing_otherworld_containment
				ireland_spirit_society_otherworld_containment
				ireland_spirit_security_otherworld_containment
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_compact }
			add_ideas = {
				ireland_spirit_governing_otherworld
				ireland_spirit_society_otherworld
				ireland_spirit_security_otherworld
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_five_provinces_pentarchy }
			add_ideas = {
				ireland_spirit_governing_five_provinces_pentarchy
				ireland_spirit_society_five_provinces_pentarchy

ireland_sync_route_advisor_lifecycle = {
	# Static advisor roles retain their scripted gates; this only dismisses an
	# already-hired advisor after an incompatible office or route transition.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = ireland_sean_lemass
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_archived
				has_country_flag = ireland_government_vacancy
				has_country_flag = ireland_office_cabinet_minister_vacant
				has_country_flag = ireland_law_constitutional_government_exit_available
				has_country_leader = { ruling_only = yes character = ireland_sean_lemass }
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_route_committed
					NOT = {
						OR = {
							has_country_flag = ireland_route_de_valera_stewardship
							has_country_flag = ireland_route_de_valera_reconciliation
							has_country_flag = ireland_route_de_valera_emergency
							has_country_flag = ireland_route_lemass_developmental
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		deactivate_advisor = ireland_sean_lemass
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = ireland_richard_mulcahy
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_archived
				has_country_flag = ireland_government_vacancy
				has_country_flag = ireland_office_chief_of_staff_vacant
				has_country_flag = ireland_military_command_collapse
				has_country_flag = ireland_law_constitutional_government_exit_available
				has_country_leader = { ruling_only = yes character = ireland_richard_mulcahy }
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_route_committed
					NOT = {
						OR = {
							has_country_flag = ireland_route_cosgrave_institutional
							has_country_flag = ireland_route_mulcahy_civil_defence
							has_country_flag = ireland_route_costello_coalition
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		deactivate_advisor = ireland_richard_mulcahy
	}
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ireland_oscar_traynor OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_archived has_country_flag = ireland_government_vacancy has_country_flag = ireland_law_constitutional_government_exit_available NOT = { ireland_route_advisor_historical_constitutional = yes } date > "1945.1.1" } } deactivate_advisor = ireland_oscar_traynor }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ireland_douglas_hyde OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_archived has_country_flag = ireland_government_vacancy has_country_flag = ireland_law_constitutional_government_exit_available NOT = { ireland_route_advisor_historical_constitutional = yes } date > "1945.6.24" } } deactivate_advisor = ireland_douglas_hyde }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ireland_sean_t_okelly OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_archived has_country_flag = ireland_government_vacancy has_country_flag = ireland_law_constitutional_government_exit_available NOT = { ireland_route_advisor_historical_constitutional = yes } NOT = { date > "1945.6.24" } } } deactivate_advisor = ireland_sean_t_okelly }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ireland_frank_aiken_advisor OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_archived has_country_flag = ireland_government_vacancy has_country_flag = ireland_office_cabinet_minister_vacant NOT = { ireland_route_advisor_de_valera_lemass = yes } } } deactivate_advisor = ireland_frank_aiken_advisor }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ireland_sean_macentee_advisor OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_archived has_country_flag = ireland_government_vacancy has_country_flag = ireland_office_cabinet_minister_vacant NOT = { ireland_route_advisor_de_valera_lemass = yes } } } deactivate_advisor = ireland_sean_macentee_advisor }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ireland_thekla_beere_advisor OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_archived has_country_flag = ireland_government_vacancy NOT = { ireland_route_advisor_de_valera_lemass = yes } } } deactivate_advisor = ireland_thekla_beere_advisor }

	if = { limit = { has_idea = ireland_joseph_macrory_advisor OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_archived has_country_flag = ireland_government_vacancy has_country_flag = ireland_office_cabinet_minister_vacant NOT = { ireland_route_advisor_clerical = yes } } } deactivate_advisor = ireland_joseph_macrory_advisor }

	# Route commitment is a leadership transition, not a second office pile-up.
	if = { limit = { has_character = IRE_Éamon_de_valera NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_de_valera_stewardship } NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_de_valera_reconciliation } NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_de_valera_emergency } } retire_character = IRE_Éamon_de_valera }

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_character = ireland_richard_mulcahy
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_cosgrave_institutional }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_mulcahy_civil_defence }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_costello_coalition }
		}

	}

ireland_activate_route_leader = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_route_committed
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_transition_complete }

			long_name = ireland_party_de_valera_stewardship_long
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_activated_de_valera_stewardship
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_de_valera_reconciliation NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_activated_de_valera_reconciliation } }

	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_ailtiri_total_state NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_activated_ailtiri_total_state } }
		# ireland_gearoid_o_cuinneagain is recruited by country history.
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ailtiri_corporate_state_ready }
			# ireland_regional_ailtiri_council is recruited by country history.
			promote_character = {
				character = ireland_regional_ailtiri_council
				ideology = fascism
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_ailtiri_corporate_state_ready } }
			# ireland_ailtiri_state_directorate is recruited by country history.
			promote_character = {
				character = ireland_ailtiri_state_directorate
				ideology = fascism
			}
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = fascism
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = fascism
			name = ireland_party_ailtiri_total_state
			long_name = ireland_party_ailtiri_total_state_long
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_activated_ailtiri_total_state
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_high_king NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_activated_high_king } }
		# ireland_high_king is recruited by country history.
		promote_character = {
			character = ireland_high_king
			ideology = despotism
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = neutrality
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = neutrality
			name = ireland_party_high_king
			long_name = ireland_party_high_king_long
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_activated_high_king
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_five_provinces NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_activated_five_provinces } }
		# ireland_five_provinces_steward is recruited by country history.
		promote_character = {
			character = ireland_five_provinces_steward
			ideology = despotism
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = neutrality
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = neutrality
			name = ireland_party_five_provinces
			long_name = ireland_party_five_provinces_long
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_activated_five_provinces
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_otherworld NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_activated_otherworld } }
		# ireland_compact_steward is recruited by country history.
		promote_character = {
			character = ireland_compact_steward
			ideology = despotism
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = neutrality
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = neutrality
			name = ireland_party_otherworld
			long_name = ireland_party_otherworld_long
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_activated_otherworld
	}
	ireland_sync_route_advisor_lifecycle = yes
}

ireland_sync_legacy_ledgers = {
	# Canonical runtime ledgers are authoritative. Mirror them into the historical
	# decision-facing names so flavour and UI consumers stay current without
	# overwriting changes made by focuses, decisions, and events.
	set_variable = { var = ireland_food_security value = ireland_provision_food }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_energy_continuity value = ireland_provision_energy }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_transport_serviceability value = ireland_provision_transport }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_maritime_access value = ireland_provision_maritime }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_command_readiness value = ireland_readiness_command }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_equipment_standard value = ireland_readiness_equipment }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_mobilisation_depth value = ireland_readiness_mobilisation }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_supply_mobility value = ireland_readiness_supply }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_coastal_air_warning value = ireland_readiness_warning }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_intelligence_coverage value = ireland_readiness_intelligence }
	# Cabinet Cohesion is an independent companion ledger; Social Consent is not
	# mirrored into it because the canonical opening deliberately separates 65
	# cabinet confidence from 55 general consent.
	set_variable = { var = ireland_unionist_consent value = ireland_northern_unionist_consent }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_labour_cooperation value = ireland_northern_labour_cooperation }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_security_stability value = ireland_northern_security_stability }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_british_flexibility value = ireland_northern_british_flexibility }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_integration_capacity value = ireland_northern_integration_capacity }
	ireland_recompute_provision = yes
	ireland_recompute_readiness = yes
	# Keep the historical decision-facing aliases current for external consumers after
	# the canonical displays have applied their caps and bottleneck floors.
	set_variable = { var = ireland_provision_display value = ireland_provision_display }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_readiness_display value = ireland_readiness_display }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_readiness_command value = ireland_readiness_command }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_readiness_equipment value = ireland_readiness_equipment }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_readiness_maritime_protection value = ireland_readiness_maritime_protection }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_readiness_intelligence value = ireland_readiness_intelligence }
}

ireland_recompute_coastwatch_posts = {
	set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_posts_established value = constant:ireland.zero }
	add_to_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_posts_established value = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_posts }
	add_to_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_posts_established value = ireland_coastwatch_sector_2_posts }
	add_to_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_posts_established value = ireland_coastwatch_sector_3_posts }
	add_to_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_posts_established value = ireland_coastwatch_sector_4_posts }
	add_to_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_posts_established value = ireland_coastwatch_sector_5_posts }
	add_to_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_posts_established value = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_posts }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = ireland_coastwatch_posts_established
		min = constant:ireland.zero
		max = constant:ireland.coastwatch_posts_total
	}
}

ireland_prepare_coastwatch_sector = {
	set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = constant:ireland.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_operational } }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_operational
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_2_operational }
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_operational
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_2_operational
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_3_operational }
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = 3 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_operational
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_2_operational
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_3_operational
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_4_operational }
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = 4 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_operational
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_2_operational
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_3_operational
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_4_operational
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_5_operational }
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_operational
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_2_operational
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_3_operational
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_4_operational
			has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_5_operational
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_operational }
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = 6 }
	}
}

ireland_record_coastwatch_sector_result = {
	# Replace any earlier partial allocation for this sector before calculating its
	# share of the fixed historical total. A partial result can be retried.
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = 1 compare = equals } } set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_posts value = constant:ireland.zero } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = 2 compare = equals } } set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_2_posts value = constant:ireland.zero } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = 3 compare = equals } } set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_3_posts value = constant:ireland.zero } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = 4 compare = equals } } set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_4_posts value = constant:ireland.zero } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = 5 compare = equals } } set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_5_posts value = constant:ireland.zero } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = 6 compare = equals } } set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_posts value = constant:ireland.zero } }
	ireland_recompute_coastwatch_posts = yes
	set_temp_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_remaining value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_posts_total }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { ireland_coastwatch_sector_remaining = ireland_coastwatch_posts_established }
	set_temp_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sectors_remaining value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_count }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { ireland_coastwatch_sectors_remaining = ireland_coastwatch_sectors_completed }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_remaining min = constant:ireland.zero max = constant:ireland.coastwatch_posts_total }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sectors_remaining min = constant:ireland.one max = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_count }
	divide_temp_variable = { ireland_coastwatch_sector_remaining = ireland_coastwatch_sectors_remaining }
	set_temp_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_partial_posts value = ireland_coastwatch_sector_remaining }
	multiply_temp_variable = { ireland_coastwatch_partial_posts = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_partial }
	divide_temp_variable = { ireland_coastwatch_partial_posts = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_full }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = 1 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = constant:ireland.mission_result_full compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_posts value = ireland_coastwatch_sector_remaining }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_post_coverage value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_full }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_observer_training value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_full }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_communications_reliability value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_full }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_recognition value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_full }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_weather_reporting value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_full }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_rescue_access value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_full }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_security_exposure value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_security_exposure_operational }
			set_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_operational
			add_to_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sectors_completed value = constant:ireland.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = constant:ireland.mission_result_partial compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_posts value = ireland_coastwatch_partial_posts }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_post_coverage value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_partial }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_observer_training value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_partial }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_communications_reliability value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_partial }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_recognition value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_partial }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_weather_reporting value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_partial }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_rescue_access value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_partial }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_security_exposure value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_security_exposure_partial }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { check_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = constant:ireland.mission_result_failure compare = equals } check_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = constant:ireland.mission_result_escalated compare = equals } } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_posts value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_post_coverage value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_observer_training value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_communications_reliability value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_recognition value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_weather_reporting value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_rescue_access value = constant:ireland.zero }

			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_2_post_coverage value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_2_observer_training value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_2_communications_reliability value = constant:ireland.zero }

			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_rescue_access value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_component_partial }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_security_exposure value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_security_exposure_partial }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { check_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = constant:ireland.mission_result_failure compare = equals } check_variable = { var = ireland_mission_result value = constant:ireland.mission_result_escalated compare = equals } } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_posts value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_post_coverage value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_observer_training value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_communications_reliability value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_recognition value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_weather_reporting value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_rescue_access value = constant:ireland.zero }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_security_exposure value = constant:ireland.coastwatch_sector_security_exposure_failure }
		}
	}
	ireland_recompute_coastwatch_posts = yes
	set_variable = { var = ireland_coastwatch_active_sector value = constant:ireland.zero }

# Current named work proofs for the Border Belt health mission. The five
# district flags are closed records from Donegal, Cavan, Leitrim, Louth, and
# Monaghan. The matched-district count is separate: jurisdiction, staff,
# funding, transport, and patient access must be earned by distinct work
# surfaces rather than inferred from a Dublin building.
ireland_sync_named_northern_objectives = {
	set_variable = { var = ireland_border_belt_districts value = constant:ireland.zero }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_flavour_f17_donegal_border_closed } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_border_belt_districts value = constant:ireland.one } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_flavour_f17_cavan_rates_closed } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_border_belt_districts value = constant:ireland.one } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_flavour_f17_leitrim_roads_closed } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_border_belt_districts value = constant:ireland.one } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_flavour_f17_louth_border_trade_closed } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_border_belt_districts value = constant:ireland.one } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_flavour_f17_monaghan_farm_security_closed } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_border_belt_districts value = constant:ireland.one } }

	set_variable = { var = ireland_northern_health_matches value = constant:ireland.zero }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_create_a_public_health_board_complete } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_northern_health_matches value = constant:ireland.one } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_open_a_border_corridor_complete } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_northern_health_matches value = constant:ireland.one } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_open_cross_border_confidence_measures_complete } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_northern_health_matches value = constant:ireland.one } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_maintain_welfare_and_pensions_complete } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_northern_health_matches value = constant:ireland.one } }
}
ireland_sync_northern_final_review_proofs = {
	# Each proof is a current, named surface. Durable mission and action history
	# remains separate from these flags so a later breach reopens the review.
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_representation_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_policing_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_courts_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_finance_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_services_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_transport_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_rights_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_resistance_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_guarantees_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_withdrawal_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_demobilisation_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_security_sustained_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_emergency_law_complete
	clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_proof_complete

	if = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = { state = 119 }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_security_stability value = constant:ireland.northern_core_security_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_failure }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ireland_northern_security_review_days value = constant:ireland.one }
	}
	else = { set_variable = { var = ireland_northern_security_review_days value = constant:ireland.zero } }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = ireland_northern_security_review_days
		min = constant:ireland.zero
		max = constant:ireland.northern_core_security_review_days
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_security_review_days value = constant:ireland.northern_core_security_review_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_security_sustained_complete
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_unionist_consent value = constant:ireland.northern_core_unionist_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_nationalist_consent value = constant:ireland.northern_core_nationalist_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_labour_cooperation value = constant:ireland.northern_core_labour_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_mission_secure_a_representative_northern_convention_full_recorded
				has_country_flag = ireland_mission_conduct_a_supervised_plebiscite_full_recorded
				has_country_flag = ireland_mission_ratify_the_federal_settlement_full_recorded
			}
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_policy_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_lawful_deferment
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_federal_prepared
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_unitary_prepared
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_wartime_bargain_complete
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_representation_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ireland_law_public_order_stage value = constant:ireland.law_stage_4 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			has_country_flag = ireland_action_vet_and_rebuild_policing_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_mission_restore_ordinary_policing_full_recorded
		NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_policing_failure }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_sectarian_policing_failure }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_policing_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_civil_service_and_courts_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_law_constitutional_government_stage value = constant:ireland.law_stage_4 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_law_public_order_stage value = constant:ireland.law_stage_4 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_crisis_active }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_overreach }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_courts_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_action_merge_tax_and_debt_administration_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_administrative_reach value = constant:ireland.northern_core_admin_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_finance_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_action_maintain_welfare_and_pensions_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_mission_open_the_cross_border_health_service_full_recorded
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_integration_capacity value = constant:ireland.northern_core_integration_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_services_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_mission_secure_the_dublin_to_belfast_corridor_full_recorded
			check_variable = { var = ireland_readiness_supply value = constant:ireland.northern_core_transport_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_transport_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_action_settle_education_and_religious_safeguards_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_mission_deliver_the_education_settlement_full_recorded
			check_variable = { var = ireland_minority_confidence value = constant:ireland.northern_core_minority_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_rights_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = { state = 119 resistance < constant:ireland.northern_core_resistance_ceiling }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_failure }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_resistance_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_action_review_foreign_guarantees_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_british_flexibility value = constant:ireland.northern_core_british_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_law_guarantee_stage value = constant:ireland.law_stage_5 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_sponsor_dependence_failure }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_permanent_foreign_veto_failure }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_guarantees_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_withdrawal_safeguard_enforceable
			has_country_flag = ireland_action_review_foreign_guarantees_complete
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_followup_access_withdrawal_closed }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_withdrawal_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_action_demobilise_and_settle_veterans_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_mission_settle_the_demobilised_forces_full_recorded
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_mobilisation_command_collapse }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_military_command_collapse }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_security_command_collapse }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_demobilisation_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_review_started }
				has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_review_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_repeal_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_law_reviewed
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_overreach }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_emergency_law_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = { state = 119 }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_occupation_ledger value = constant:ireland.northern_occupation_stage compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_settlement_ledger value = constant:ireland.northern_settlement_stage_services compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_integration_capacity value = constant:ireland.northern_core_integration_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_unionist_consent value = constant:ireland.northern_core_unionist_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_nationalist_consent value = constant:ireland.northern_core_nationalist_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_labour_cooperation value = constant:ireland.northern_core_labour_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_british_flexibility value = constant:ireland.northern_core_british_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_representation_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_policing_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_courts_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_finance_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_services_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_transport_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_rights_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_resistance_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_guarantees_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_withdrawal_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_demobilisation_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_security_sustained_complete
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_emergency_law_complete
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_failure }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_proof_complete
	}
}

ireland_sync_northern_ledger = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = ireland_northern_control_candidate value = constant:ireland.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { any_controlled_state = { state = 119 } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = ireland_northern_control_candidate value = constant:ireland.one }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_northern_control_ledger value = constant:ireland.one }
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_control_ledger_active
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { var = ireland_northern_control_ledger value = constant:ireland.zero }
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_control_ledger_active
	}
	# Compatibility projection for older occupation gates. The authored
	# transitional-authority completion flag is canonical; this alias exists
	# only for legacy action availability and is rebuilt on every sync.
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_establish_a_transitional_authority_complete }
		set_country_flag = ireland_provisional_northern_authority
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_provisional_northern_authority }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_control_candidate value = constant:ireland.one compare = equals }
			OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_war_plan has_country_flag = ireland_northern_wartime_bargain_complete has_country_flag = ireland_occupation_active has_country_flag = ireland_provisional_northern_authority has_country_flag = ireland_integration_review }
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_northern_occupation_ledger value = constant:ireland.northern_occupation_stage }
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_ledger_active
		set_country_flag = ireland_occupation_active
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_active
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { var = ireland_northern_occupation_ledger value = constant:ireland.zero }
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_ledger_active
		clr_country_flag = ireland_occupation_active
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_active
		clr_country_flag = ireland_provisional_northern_authority
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_active }
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_was_active
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_control_candidate value = constant:ireland.zero compare = equals }
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_was_active
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_integration_complete }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_failure
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_control_candidate value = constant:ireland.one compare = equals } }
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_failure
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_control_candidate value = constant:ireland.one compare = equals }
			OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_local_authorities_complete has_country_flag = ireland_northern_contact has_country_flag = ireland_northern_trade_corridor_complete }
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_northern_settlement_candidate value = constant:ireland.northern_settlement_stage_local }
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { var = ireland_northern_settlement_candidate value = constant:ireland.zero } }
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_labour_social_bridge_complete has_country_flag = ireland_northern_rights_framework_complete has_country_flag = ireland_northern_education_compact_complete } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = ireland_northern_settlement_candidate value = constant:ireland.northern_settlement_stage_rights }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_federal_preparation_complete has_country_flag = ireland_northern_unitary_preparation_complete has_country_flag = ireland_northern_recovery_bridge_complete has_country_flag = ireland_northern_wartime_bargain_complete } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = ireland_northern_settlement_candidate value = constant:ireland.northern_settlement_stage_services }
	}
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_regional_review_complete } set_temp_variable = { var = ireland_northern_settlement_candidate value = constant:ireland.northern_settlement_stage_complete } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_settlement_candidate value = ireland_northern_settlement_ledger compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_northern_settlement_ledger value = ireland_northern_settlement_candidate }
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = ireland_northern_settlement_ledger min = constant:ireland.northern_ledger_min max = constant:ireland.northern_ledger_max }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_control_candidate value = constant:ireland.one compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_settlement_ledger value = constant:ireland.northern_settlement_stage_local compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_security_stability value = constant:ireland.northern_settlement_security_failure compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_british_flexibility value = constant:ireland.northern_settlement_british_failure compare = less_than }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_control_candidate value = constant:ireland.one compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_security_stability value = constant:ireland.northern_settlement_security_failure compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_british_flexibility value = constant:ireland.northern_settlement_british_failure compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure
	}
	ireland_sync_northern_final_review_proofs = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_proof_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_occupation_ledger value = constant:ireland.northern_occupation_stage compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_settlement_ledger value = constant:ireland.northern_settlement_stage_services compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			any_controlled_state = { state = 119 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_failure }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure }
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_northern_integration_stage value = constant:ireland.northern_integration_stage_complete }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_northern_integration_ledger value = constant:ireland.northern_integration_stage_complete }
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_integration_ledger_active
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_integration_active
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_control_candidate value = constant:ireland.one compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_settlement_ledger value = constant:ireland.northern_settlement_stage_services compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_integration_stage value = constant:ireland.northern_integration_stage_complete compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_proof_complete
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_failure }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_regional_review_ready
		set_country_flag = ireland_integration_review
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_regional_review_ready
		clr_country_flag = ireland_integration_review
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_regional_review_started
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_requested
			}
			has_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_proof_complete
			check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_integration_stage value = constant:ireland.northern_stage_core_eligible compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			any_controlled_state = { state = 119 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_failure }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_regional_review_complete
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_integration_complete
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_passed
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_stage_review
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_ledger_complete
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_core_review_open
		set_variable = { var = ireland_northern_settlement_ledger value = constant:ireland.northern_settlement_stage_complete }
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_review_requested
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_core_granted } }
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_regional_review_complete
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_integration_complete
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_passed
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_ledger_complete
	}
	ireland_apply_northern_core_review = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = ireland_northern_gui_dirty value = constant:ireland.one }
}

ireland_sync_ai_overlays = {
	# Temporary overlays are derived state. They are never a second route and
	# are cleared automatically when the triggering crisis or review closes.
	# Compatibility projections below keep older consumers synchronized with
	# canonical route, office, and emergency state. They are not independent
	# route selectors and are rebuilt every daily runtime pass.
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_war = yes
				has_country_flag = ireland_national_emergency_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_external_emergency
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_invasion_danger
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_invasion_danger }
	if = { limit = { ireland_reveal_category_regional = yes } set_country_flag = ireland_regional_order_gate }
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_regional_order_gate }
	if = { limit = { ireland_reveal_regional_celtic = yes } set_country_flag = ireland_celtic_route }
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_celtic_route }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_ira_republican } set_country_flag = ireland_ira_route }
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_ira_route }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_office_vacancy }
		set_country_flag = ireland_government_vacancy
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_government_vacancy }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_election_calendar_ready
				has_country_flag = ireland_government_formation_ready
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_overlay_election_and_government_formation
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_overlay_election_and_government_formation }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_crisis_active } set_country_flag = ireland_overlay_constitutional_crisis }
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_overlay_constitutional_crisis }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_provision_severe_bottleneck
				has_country_flag = ireland_provision_dual_bottleneck
				has_country_flag = ireland_provision_zero_component
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_overlay_provision_breakdown
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_overlay_provision_breakdown }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_invasion_danger
				has_war = yes
				has_country_flag = ireland_national_emergency_active
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_overlay_invasion_danger
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_overlay_invasion_danger }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_war = yes
				has_country_flag = ireland_national_emergency_active
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_overlay_declared_war_and_home_defence
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_overlay_declared_war_and_home_defence }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_integration_active
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_overlay_northern_occupation_and_integration
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_overlay_northern_occupation_and_integration }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = ireland_sponsor_dependence value = constant:ireland.achievement_leverage_ceiling compare = greater_than }
				has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_fr05_german_sponsor_collapse_refused
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_overlay_foreign_sponsor_collapse
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_overlay_foreign_sponsor_collapse }
	# A sustained maximum source leverage is a durable dependency failure only
	# after an authored sponsor agreement exists. The overlay remains derived;
	# this history flag feeds law failure, route disqualification, AI recovery,
	# and the explicit termination action.
	if = {
		limit = {
			ireland_sponsor_agreement_recorded = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = {
					var = ireland_sponsor_dependence
					value = constant:ireland.achievement_leverage_ceiling
					compare = greater_than
				}
				has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_fr05_german_sponsor_collapse_refused
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_sponsor_dependence_failure
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_route_committed
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_transition_complete }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_overlay_leadership_and_office_vacancy
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_overlay_leadership_and_office_vacancy }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_postwar_review_started } set_country_flag = ireland_overlay_postwar_demobilisation_and_reconstruction }
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_overlay_postwar_demobilisation_and_reconstruction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_due
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_overlay_route_conclusion_review_and_consolidation
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_overlay_route_conclusion_review_and_consolidation }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_convergence_order_capstone_complete }
		set_country_flag = ireland_convergence_verified
	}
}

ireland_apply_northern_core_review = {
	if = {
		limit = { ireland_can_northern_core_review = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_core_granted } }
		119 = { add_core_of = ROOT }
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_core_granted
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_core_eligibility_confirmed
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_core_review_blocked
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_passed NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_core_granted } NOT = { any_controlled_state = { state = 119 } } }
		set_country_flag = ireland_northern_core_review_blocked
	}
}

ireland_sync_ai_decision_utility = {
	# Translate live route safety, deadline, mission-result, and capacity state
	# into a small reusable scoring layer. This is deliberately read-only: route
	# gates, action costs, mission outcomes, escalation, and cleanup remain in
	# their owning decision and effect blocks.
	set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_social_consent_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_utility_social_consent_floor_prudent }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_foreign_exposure_ceiling value = constant:ireland.ai_utility_foreign_exposure_ceiling_prudent }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_lemass_developmental
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_costello_coalition
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_de_valera_reconciliation
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_clann_agrarian_broker
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_producer_council_state
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_congress_plural
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_social_consent_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_utility_social_consent_floor_pragmatic }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_foreign_exposure_ceiling value = constant:ireland.ai_utility_foreign_exposure_ceiling_pragmatic }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_ira_civil_republican
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_ira_ghq_command
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_congress_vanguard
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_oduffy_personal
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_oduffy_corporate
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_ailtiri_total_state
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_high_king_sacral
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_dominion
				has_country_flag = ireland_profile_convergence_order
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_social_consent_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_utility_social_consent_floor_radical }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_foreign_exposure_ceiling value = constant:ireland.ai_utility_foreign_exposure_ceiling_radical }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_national_emergency_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_external_emergency
				has_country_flag = ireland_invasion_danger
				has_war = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_social_consent_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_utility_social_consent_floor_survival }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_foreign_exposure_ceiling value = constant:ireland.ai_utility_foreign_exposure_ceiling_survival }
	}

	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_deadline_active
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_dependency_needed
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_conclusion_needed
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_material_safety_breached
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_material_ready
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_social_consent_breached
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_foreign_exposure_breached

	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_election_calendar_ready
				has_country_flag = ireland_government_formation_ready
				has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_crisis_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_review_due
				has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_due
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_route_review_active
					check_variable = { var = ireland_route_review_days value = constant:ireland.ai_utility_deadline_window_days compare = less_than }
				}
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_active
					check_variable = { var = ireland_conclusion_review_days value = constant:ireland.ai_utility_deadline_window_days compare = less_than }
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_deadline_active
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_critical_component_slot_needed
				has_country_flag = ireland_invasion_slot_needed
				has_country_flag = ireland_neutrality_incident_slot_needed
				has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_slot_needed
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_slot_needed
				has_country_flag = ireland_occupation_slot_needed
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_review_slot_needed
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_dependency_needed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_review_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_review_due
				has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_due
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_partial
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_conclusion_needed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_failed
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_partial
				has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure
				has_country_flag = ireland_national_emergency_active
				has_country_flag = ireland_external_emergency
				has_country_flag = ireland_military_command_collapse
				has_country_flag = ireland_security_command_collapse
				has_country_flag = ireland_mobilisation_command_collapse
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = ireland_missions_failed value = constant:ireland.zero compare = greater_than }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure_recovered }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = ireland_missions_escalated value = constant:ireland.zero compare = greater_than }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure_recovered }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = ireland_missions_partial value = constant:ireland.zero compare = greater_than }
					NOT = { ireland_has_route_conclusion_capstone = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_provision_severe_bottleneck
				has_country_flag = ireland_provision_dual_bottleneck
				has_country_flag = ireland_provision_zero_component
				has_country_flag = ireland_military_command_collapse
				has_country_flag = ireland_mobilisation_command_collapse
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_material_safety_breached
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_provision_severe_bottleneck
					has_country_flag = ireland_provision_dual_bottleneck
					has_country_flag = ireland_provision_zero_component
					has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_equipment_bottleneck
					has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_supply_bottleneck
					has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_warning_bottleneck
					has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_maritime_bottleneck
				}
			}
			check_variable = { var = ireland_provision_display value = constant:ireland.provision_severe_cap compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ireland_readiness_display value = constant:ireland.readiness_provision_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_material_ready
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_social_consent value = ireland_ai_social_consent_floor compare = less_than } }
		set_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_social_consent_breached
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_foreign_exposure value = ireland_ai_foreign_exposure_ceiling compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_foreign_exposure_breached
	}
}

# Translate the durable profile roster and the ten temporary overlays into the
# additive domain layer used by decision and mission queue scoring. These flags
# never open content or bypass a gate; they only express the matrix's primary
# and supporting category bonuses.
ireland_sync_ai_domain_flags = {
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_settlement_government
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_elections
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_organised_society
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_cultural_archival
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_radical_underground
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_provision
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_readiness
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_neutrality_incidents
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_foreign
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_northern
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_occupation_integration
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_regional_external
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_postwar
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_settlement_government
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_elections
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_organised_society
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_cultural_archival
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_radical_underground
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_provision
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_readiness
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_neutrality_incidents
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_foreign
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_northern
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_occupation_integration
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_regional_external
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_postwar

	# Durable profile defaults. Recovery preserves the profile flag and therefore
	# preserves these priorities even when the current office or leader changes.
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_historical_de_valera } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_provision set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_neutrality_incidents }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_de_valera_reconciliation } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_organised_society set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_radical_underground }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_de_valera_emergency } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_provision set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_readiness set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_neutrality_incidents set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_settlement_government }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_lemass_developmental } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_provision set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_organised_society set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_regional_external }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_cosgrave_institutional } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_provision set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_organised_society }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_mulcahy_civil_defence } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_readiness set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_neutrality_incidents }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_costello_coalition } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_elections set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_organised_society }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_dillon_intervention } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_readiness set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_foreign set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_elections set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_neutrality_incidents }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_norton_parliamentary } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_organised_society set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_provision set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_settlement_government }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_clann_agrarian_broker } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_organised_society set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_provision set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_regional_external set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_settlement_government }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_constitutional_vocational } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_organised_society set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_provision }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_serious_cultural_stewardship } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_cultural_archival set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_organised_society set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_regional_external }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_ira_civil_republican } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_radical_underground set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_organised_society }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_ira_ghq_command } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_radical_underground set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_readiness set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_provision set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_neutrality_incidents }
