	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_ailtiri_total_state } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_radical_underground set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_organised_society set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_readiness }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_high_king_constitutional } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_cultural_archival set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_regional_external }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_high_king_sacral } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_cultural_archival set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_regional_external set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_radical_underground }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_five_provinces_federal } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_regional_external set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_northern set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_occupation_integration set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_settlement_government }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_five_provinces_pentarchy } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_regional_external set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_occupation_integration set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_northern set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_cultural_archival }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_compact } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_cultural_archival set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_regional_external set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_foreign }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_containment } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_cultural_archival set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_readiness set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_foreign }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_dominion } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_regional_external set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_cultural_archival set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_radical_underground set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_occupation_integration }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_convergence_order } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_regional_external set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_cultural_archival set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_radical_underground set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_occupation_integration set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_postwar }

	# Temporary overlays stack on the durable profile while an urgent state is
	# active. They can add a second primary domain without changing the route.
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_election_and_government_formation } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_elections set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_settlement_government }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_constitutional_crisis } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_elections set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_radical_underground }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_provision_breakdown } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_provision set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_readiness set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_neutrality_incidents }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_invasion_danger } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_readiness set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_neutrality_incidents set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_occupation_integration }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_declared_war_and_home_defence } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_readiness set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_provision set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_neutrality_incidents set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_occupation_integration }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_northern_occupation_and_integration } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_occupation_integration set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_northern set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_readiness set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_regional_external }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_foreign_sponsor_collapse } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_foreign set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_neutrality_incidents set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_provision }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_leadership_and_office_vacancy } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_elections set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_organised_society }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_postwar_demobilisation_and_reconstruction } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_postwar set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_provision set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_readiness set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_foreign }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_route_conclusion_review_and_consolidation } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_primary_postwar set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_foreign set_country_flag = ireland_ai_supporting_occupation_integration }
}

# Build the live candidate ledger consumed by all decision and mission AI blocks.
# Category priority is not enough on its own: each category records the active
# cause, the named repair pressure, and the dependency that makes a candidate
# worth a scarce slot. The profile stance then selects the exact normal/crisis
# willingness floors from the AI matrix.
ireland_sync_ai_candidate_ledger = {
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_cautious_constitutional
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_pragmatic_constitutional
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_radical_survival
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_authoritarian_command
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_concealed_stewardship
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_desperate_self_destructive
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_crisis_utility_mode
	set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_willingness_normal_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_willingness_normal_cautious }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_willingness_crisis_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_willingness_crisis_cautious }
	set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_environment_utility value = constant:ireland.zero }

	# Clear the thirteen three-part candidate ledger before deriving this tick's
	# state. These flags are AI-only and never open a route or grant a resource.
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_settlement_government
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_settlement_government
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_settlement_government
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_elections
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_elections
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_elections
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_organised_society
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_organised_society
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_organised_society
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_cultural_archival
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_cultural_archival
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_cultural_archival
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_radical_underground
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_radical_underground
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_radical_underground
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_provision
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_provision
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_provision
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_readiness
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_readiness
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_readiness
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_neutrality_incidents
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_neutrality_incidents
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_neutrality_incidents
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_foreign
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_foreign
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_foreign
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_northern
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_northern
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_northern
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_occupation_integration
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_occupation_integration
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_occupation_integration
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_regional_external
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_regional_external
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_regional_external
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_postwar
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_postwar
	clr_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_postwar

	# The durable profile roster is mapped to the six matrix stances. Temporary
	# overlays change urgency below without replacing the route's durable stance.
	if = { limit = { OR = {
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_historical_de_valera
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_de_valera_reconciliation
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_cosgrave_institutional
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_mulcahy_civil_defence
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_dillon_intervention
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_constitutional_vocational
	} } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_cautious_constitutional }
	if = { limit = { OR = {
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_lemass_developmental
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_costello_coalition
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_norton_parliamentary
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_clann_agrarian_broker
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_emergency_admin_restoration
	} } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_pragmatic_constitutional }
	if = { limit = { OR = {
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_de_valera_emergency
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_ira_civil_republican
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_congress_plural
	} } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_radical_survival }
	if = { limit = { OR = {
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_ira_ghq_command
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_congress_vanguard
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_oduffy_personal
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_oduffy_corporate
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_clerical_guardianship
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_producer_council_state
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_ailtiri_total_state
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_dominion
	} } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_authoritarian_command }
	if = { limit = { OR = {
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_serious_cultural_stewardship
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_high_king_constitutional
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_high_king_sacral
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_five_provinces_federal
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_five_provinces_pentarchy
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_compact
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_containment
		has_country_flag = ireland_profile_convergence_order
	} } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_concealed_stewardship }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_desperate_self_destructive_variant } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_desperate_self_destructive }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = {
		has_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_cautious_constitutional
		has_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_pragmatic_constitutional
		has_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_radical_survival
		has_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_authoritarian_command
		has_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_concealed_stewardship
		has_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_desperate_self_destructive
	} } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_cautious_constitutional }

	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_pragmatic_constitutional } set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_willingness_normal_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_willingness_normal_pragmatic } set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_willingness_crisis_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_willingness_crisis_pragmatic } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_radical_survival } set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_willingness_normal_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_willingness_normal_radical } set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_willingness_crisis_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_willingness_crisis_radical } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_authoritarian_command } set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_willingness_normal_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_willingness_normal_authoritarian } set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_willingness_crisis_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_willingness_crisis_authoritarian } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_concealed_stewardship } set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_willingness_normal_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_willingness_normal_concealed } set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_willingness_crisis_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_willingness_crisis_concealed } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_stance_desperate_self_destructive } set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_willingness_normal_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_willingness_normal_desperate } set_variable = { var = ireland_ai_willingness_crisis_floor value = constant:ireland.ai_willingness_crisis_desperate } }

	if = { limit = { OR = {
		has_country_flag = ireland_national_emergency_active
		has_country_flag = ireland_external_emergency
		has_country_flag = ireland_invasion_danger
		has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_declared_war_and_home_defence
		has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_northern_occupation_and_integration
		has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_foreign_sponsor_collapse
		has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_leadership_and_office_vacancy
		has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_route_conclusion_review_and_consolidation
		has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_deadline_active
		has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed
		has_country_flag = ireland_critical_slot_reserved
	} } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_crisis_utility_mode }

	# Government and election candidates.
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_election_calendar_ready has_country_flag = ireland_government_formation_ready has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_crisis_active has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_leadership_and_office_vacancy has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_election_and_government_formation } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_elections }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_failed has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_leadership_and_office_vacancy has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_crisis_active } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_elections }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_route_review_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_election_calendar_ready has_country_flag = ireland_government_formation_ready has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_leadership_and_office_vacancy } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_settlement_government set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_elections }

	# Social institutions and cultural evidence candidates.
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_social_consent_breached has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_provision_breakdown has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_leadership_and_office_vacancy has_country_flag = ireland_profile_norton_parliamentary has_country_flag = ireland_profile_clann_agrarian_broker has_country_flag = ireland_profile_constitutional_vocational has_country_flag = ireland_profile_clerical_guardianship has_country_flag = ireland_profile_producer_council_state has_country_flag = ireland_profile_congress_plural } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_organised_society }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_partial has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_social_consent_breached } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_organised_society }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_critical_component_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_leadership_and_office_vacancy } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_organised_society }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_serious_culture_active has_country_flag = ireland_archive_lane_open has_country_flag = ireland_hidden_evidence_gate has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_route_conclusion_review_and_consolidation has_country_flag = ireland_profile_serious_cultural_stewardship has_country_flag = ireland_profile_high_king_constitutional has_country_flag = ireland_profile_five_provinces_federal has_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_compact } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_cultural_archival }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_partial has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_route_conclusion_review_and_consolidation } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_cultural_archival }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_review_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_route_review_active has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_active has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_due has_country_flag = ireland_hidden_evidence_gate } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_cultural_archival }

	# Radical and underground candidates remain crisis- and route-bound.
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_crisis_active has_country_flag = ireland_invasion_danger has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_declared_war_and_home_defence has_country_flag = ireland_profile_ira_civil_republican has_country_flag = ireland_profile_ira_ghq_command has_country_flag = ireland_profile_congress_vanguard has_country_flag = ireland_profile_oduffy_personal has_country_flag = ireland_profile_oduffy_corporate has_country_flag = ireland_profile_ailtiri_total_state } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_radical_underground }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure has_country_flag = ireland_military_command_collapse has_country_flag = ireland_security_command_collapse has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_constitutional_crisis } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_radical_underground }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_critical_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_invasion_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_leadership_and_office_vacancy } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_radical_underground }

	# Provision and readiness candidates follow physical bottlenecks rather than
	# political preference alone.
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_provision_severe_bottleneck has_country_flag = ireland_provision_dual_bottleneck has_country_flag = ireland_provision_zero_component has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_provision_breakdown has_country_flag = ireland_national_emergency_active has_country_flag = ireland_critical_component_slot_needed } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_provision }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed has_country_flag = ireland_provision_severe_bottleneck has_country_flag = ireland_provision_dual_bottleneck has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_provision_breakdown } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_provision }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_critical_component_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_invasion_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_provision_breakdown has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_postwar_demobilisation_and_reconstruction } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_provision }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_equipment_bottleneck has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_supply_bottleneck has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_warning_bottleneck has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_maritime_bottleneck has_country_flag = ireland_invasion_danger has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_declared_war_and_home_defence has_country_flag = ireland_military_command_collapse has_country_flag = ireland_security_command_collapse has_country_flag = ireland_mobilisation_command_collapse } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_readiness }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_equipment_bottleneck has_country_flag = ireland_readiness_supply_bottleneck has_country_flag = ireland_military_command_collapse has_country_flag = ireland_mobilisation_command_collapse } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_readiness }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_invasion_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_occupation_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_critical_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_declared_war_and_home_defence } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_readiness }

	# Neutrality and foreign candidates keep incidents, access and sponsor debt
	# distinct even when both are active in the same week.
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_neutrality_incident_active has_country_flag = ireland_neutrality_incident_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_window_active has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_invasion_danger has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_foreign_sponsor_collapse } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_neutrality_incidents }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_digest_required has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_foreign_sponsor_collapse } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_neutrality_incidents }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_neutrality_incident_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_critical_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_invasion_danger } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_neutrality_incidents }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_window_active has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_foreign_sponsor_collapse has_country_flag = ireland_sponsor_dependence_failure has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_digest_required } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_foreign }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure has_country_flag = ireland_sponsor_dependence_failure has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_foreign_sponsor_collapse } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_foreign }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_window_active has_country_flag = ireland_critical_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_foreign_sponsor_collapse } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_foreign }

	# Northern work and occupation remain separate queues. Occupation repair does
	# not imply a claim, settlement, integration stage, or core.
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_active has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_northern_occupation_and_integration has_country_flag = ireland_profile_five_provinces_federal has_country_flag = ireland_profile_five_provinces_pentarchy } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_northern }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_northern_occupation_and_integration has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_northern }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_route_review_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_active has_country_flag = ireland_occupation_slot_needed } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_northern }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_northern_occupation_and_integration has_country_flag = ireland_northern_occupation_active has_country_flag = ireland_northern_integration_active has_country_flag = ireland_occupation_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_active } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_occupation_integration }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_northern_occupation_and_integration has_country_flag = ireland_northern_settlement_failure has_country_flag = ireland_route_failure } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_occupation_integration }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_occupation_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_northern_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_active has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_northern_occupation_and_integration } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_occupation_integration }

	# Regional and postwar candidates are conclusion-bound and never substitute
	# for the Northern or foreign ledgers.
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_profile_five_provinces_federal has_country_flag = ireland_profile_five_provinces_pentarchy has_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_compact has_country_flag = ireland_profile_otherworld_dominion has_country_flag = ireland_profile_convergence_order has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_route_conclusion_review_and_consolidation has_country_flag = ireland_regional_order_gate } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_regional_external }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_partial has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_failed has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_route_conclusion_review_and_consolidation } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_regional_external }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_review_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_active has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_due has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_route_conclusion_review_and_consolidation } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_regional_external }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_postwar_demobilisation_and_reconstruction has_country_flag = ireland_postwar_review_started has_country_flag = ireland_major_war_ended has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_route_conclusion_review_and_consolidation has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_due } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_cause_postwar }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_postwar_demobilisation_and_reconstruction has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_partial has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_failed } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_repair_postwar }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_review_slot_needed has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_active has_country_flag = ireland_conclusion_review_due has_country_flag = ireland_overlay_postwar_demobilisation_and_reconstruction } } set_country_flag = ireland_ai_candidate_dependency_postwar }

	# Turn the shared environment into the matrix's expected-utility envelope.
	# Candidate-specific domain bonuses and burden penalties remain in each
	# authored ai_will_do block; this envelope supplies the profile floor.
	if = { limit = { ireland_ai_utility_any_candidate_signal = yes } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_ai_environment_utility value = constant:ireland.ai_candidate_pressure_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_deadline_active } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_ai_environment_utility value = constant:ireland.ai_deadline_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_dependency_needed } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_ai_environment_utility value = constant:ireland.ai_route_conclusion_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_recovery_needed } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_ai_environment_utility value = constant:ireland.ai_recovery_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_material_ready } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_ai_environment_utility value = constant:ireland.ai_material_ready_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_material_safety_breached } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_ai_environment_utility value = constant:ireland.ai_material_floor_penalty } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_foreign_exposure_breached } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_ai_environment_utility value = constant:ireland.ai_foreign_ceiling_penalty } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_ai_utility_social_consent_breached } add_to_variable = { var = ireland_ai_environment_utility value = constant:ireland.ai_social_consent_floor_penalty } }
}

ireland_update_achievement_trackers = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_initialized }
		# Stakeholder ledgers are durable mirrors of the live route systems. Each milestone is
		# applied once, so recovery cannot erase the history used by the achievement matrix.
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_costello_coalition_complete NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coalition_charter_recorded } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_coalition_cohesion = 10 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coalition_charter_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_written_coalition_charter_complete NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_written_coalition_charter_recorded } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_coalition_cohesion = 5 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_written_coalition_charter_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_maintain_cabinet_discipline_full_recorded NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_cabinet_discipline_recorded } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_coalition_cohesion = 5 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_cabinet_discipline_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_union_local_mandate_complete NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_union_mandate_recorded } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_union_commitment = 10 }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_movement_unity = 5 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_union_mandate_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_labour_as_conscience_complete NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_labour_conscience_recorded } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_union_commitment = 5 }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_movement_unity = 5 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_labour_conscience_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_labour_social_law_complete NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_labour_social_law_recorded } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_union_commitment = 5 }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_movement_unity = 5 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_labour_social_law_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_prepare_a_wartime_unity_bargain_complete NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_wartime_unity_recorded } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_union_commitment = 5 }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_movement_unity = 5 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_wartime_unity_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_agrarian_support_bloc_complete NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_agrarian_bloc_recorded } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_agrarian_cohesion = 10 }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_rural_mandate = 5 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_agrarian_bloc_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_clann_by_election_complete NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_clann_election_recorded } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_agrarian_cohesion = 5 }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_rural_mandate = 10 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_clann_election_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_recognise_an_agrarian_support_bloc_complete NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_agrarian_recognition_recorded } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_rural_mandate = 5 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_agrarian_recognition_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_professional_defence_council_complete NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_professional_defence_recorded } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_army_acceptance = 20 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_professional_defence_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_army_acceptance_complete NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_army_acceptance_recorded } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_army_acceptance = 30 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_army_acceptance_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_dillon_safeguards_ready NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_dillon_army_recorded } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_army_acceptance = 10 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_dillon_army_recorded
		}
		# Sponsor dependence is the maximum live leverage, not a passive stockpile.
		set_variable = { var = ireland_sponsor_dependence value = ireland_british_leverage }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_vatican_leverage value = ireland_sponsor_dependence compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_sponsor_dependence value = ireland_vatican_leverage } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_american_leverage value = ireland_sponsor_dependence compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_sponsor_dependence value = ireland_american_leverage } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_german_leverage value = ireland_sponsor_dependence compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { var = ireland_sponsor_dependence value = ireland_german_leverage } }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_sponsor_dependence value = ireland_sponsor_dependence }
		# The Atlantic league is earned through a charter, a defence proposal, a ratified
		# regional mission, and four separately recorded sovereign contribution partners.
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_action_draft_an_atlantic_consultation_charter_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_action_propose_a_regional_defence_league_complete
				has_country_flag = ireland_mission_ratify_a_regional_charter_full_recorded
				ireland_atlantic_sovereign_members_valid = yes
				has_country_flag = ireland_mission_meet_the_member_contribution_schedule_full_recorded
			OR = {
				has_war = yes
				has_country_flag = ireland_invasion_danger
				ireland_has_active_neutrality_incident = yes
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_provision_display
				value = constant:ireland.regional_defence_league_provision_floor
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_readiness_display
				value = constant:ireland.regional_defence_league_readiness_floor
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_provision_maritime
				value = constant:ireland.atlantic_defence_order_maritime_provision_floor
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_provision_transport
				value = constant:ireland.atlantic_defence_order_transport_provision_floor
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_readiness_warning
				value = constant:ireland.regional_defence_league_warning_floor
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_readiness_maritime_protection
				value = constant:ireland.regional_defence_league_maritime_floor
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_readiness_command
				value = constant:ireland.regional_defence_league_command_floor
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_readiness_intelligence
				value = constant:ireland.regional_defence_league_intelligence_floor
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_sponsor_dependence_failure }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_access_failure }
		}
			set_country_flag = ireland_atlantic_route
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_atlantic_league_formed
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_atlantic_route
				has_country_flag = ireland_british_channel_open
				ireland_british_partner_valid = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_atlantic_member_1_recorded }
			}
			add_to_variable = { ireland_achievement_atlantic_league_sovereign_members = 1 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_atlantic_member_1_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_atlantic_route
				has_country_flag = ireland_american_channel_open
				ireland_american_partner_valid = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_atlantic_member_2_recorded }
			}
			add_to_variable = { ireland_achievement_atlantic_league_sovereign_members = 1 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_atlantic_member_2_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_atlantic_route
				has_country_flag = ireland_german_channel_open
				ireland_german_partner_valid = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_atlantic_member_3_recorded }
			}
			add_to_variable = { ireland_achievement_atlantic_league_sovereign_members = 1 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_atlantic_member_3_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_atlantic_route
				has_country_flag = ireland_vatican_channel_open
				ireland_vatican_partner_valid = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_atlantic_member_4_recorded }
			}
			add_to_variable = { ireland_achievement_atlantic_league_sovereign_members = 1 }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_atlantic_member_4_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_atlantic_route has_country_flag = ireland_action_offer_a_protectorate_treaty_complete }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_subjects_counted_as_sovereign
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_paper_members
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_atlantic_route
				check_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_atlantic_league_sovereign_members value = 4 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				has_country_flag = ireland_mission_meet_the_member_contribution_schedule_full_recorded
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_subjects_counted_as_sovereign }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_common_defence_unpaid }
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_atlantic_contributions_funded
			add_to_variable = { ireland_achievement_atlantic_cohesion_days = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_mission_meet_the_member_contribution_schedule_failure_recorded
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_atlantic_route
					has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_atlantic_league_formed
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_common_defence_unpaid
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_atlantic_cohesion_days value = 1095 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_atlantic_league_cohesion_three_years
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_congress }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_coalition_cohesion value = ireland_congress_coalition_cohesion }
		}
		else = { set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_coalition_cohesion value = ireland_coalition_cohesion } }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_union_commitment value = ireland_union_commitment }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_rural_mandate value = ireland_rural_mandate }
		set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_army_acceptance value = ireland_army_acceptance }
		# Durable extrema record dependency, neutrality, and foreign exposure even after recovery.
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_british_leverage value = ireland_achievement_british_leverage_max compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_british_leverage_max value = ireland_british_leverage }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_vatican_leverage value = ireland_achievement_vatican_leverage_max compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_vatican_leverage_max value = ireland_vatican_leverage }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_american_leverage value = ireland_achievement_american_leverage_max compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_american_leverage_max value = ireland_american_leverage }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_german_leverage value = ireland_achievement_german_leverage_max compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_german_leverage_max value = ireland_german_leverage }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_neutrality_integrity value = ireland_achievement_neutrality_integrity_min compare = less_than } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_neutrality_integrity_min value = ireland_neutrality_integrity }
		}
		# A-03 is updated by the partner/channel ledger helper before this
		# tracker runs; visible reaction count is deliberately not a proxy for
		# cooperation depth.

		# The constitutional achievement snapshots confidence at the dated commencement event.
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_h01_accepted }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_1937_constitution_passed
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_constitution_commencement_recorded } }
				set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_constitution_approval value = ireland_social_consent }
				set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_minority_confidence_at_commencement value = ireland_minority_confidence }
				set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_constitution_commencement_recorded
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_minority_confidence value = constant:ireland.achievement_floor_60 compare = less_than } }
					set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_minority_confidence_below_60_at_commencement
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_h01_deferred }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_constitution_rejected
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_repeal_complete
					has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_calendar_restored
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_emergency_ended
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_ordinary_law_restored
		}

		# Coastwatch achievement history mirrors only the six operational sector records.
		# Air warning, harbour defence, and marine patrol work remain separate systems.
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_1_operational } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_sector_1_operational }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_2_operational } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_sector_2_operational }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_3_operational } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_sector_3_operational }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_4_operational } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_sector_4_operational }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_5_operational } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_sector_5_operational }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_coastwatch_sector_6_operational } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_sector_6_operational }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_sector_1_operational
				has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_sector_2_operational
				has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_sector_3_operational
				has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_sector_4_operational
				has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_sector_5_operational
				has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_sector_6_operational
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_national_emergency_active
					has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_opening_complete
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_readiness_warning value = constant:ireland.readiness_warning_bottleneck compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				add_to_variable = { ireland_achievement_coastwatch_warning_floor_streak = constant:ireland.one }
				add_to_variable = { ireland_achievement_coastwatch_active_days = constant:ireland.one }
			}
			else = { set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_warning_floor_streak value = constant:ireland.zero } }
		}
		else = { set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_warning_floor_streak value = constant:ireland.zero } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_warning_floor_streak value = constant:ireland.achievement_coastwatch_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_coastwatch_streak_during_emergency
		}

		# Three named full-success rescue records count once each.
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_investigate_a_merchant_sinking_full_recorded NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_rescue_record_1 } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_achievement_rescue_recovery_missions_completed = constant:ireland.one }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_rescue_record_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_replace_a_lost_merchant_vessel_full_recorded NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_rescue_record_2 } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_achievement_rescue_recovery_missions_completed = constant:ireland.one }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_rescue_record_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_mission_resolve_an_aircrew_internment_case_full_recorded NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_rescue_record_3 } }
			add_to_variable = { ireland_achievement_rescue_recovery_missions_completed = constant:ireland.one }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_rescue_record_3
		}

		# Major war completion samples maritime access at the actual transition.
		if = { limit = { has_war = yes } set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_war_was_active value = constant:ireland.one } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_war = yes }
				check_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_war_was_active value = constant:ireland.one compare = equals }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_major_war_finished }
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_major_war_finished
			set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_maritime_access_at_major_war_end value = ireland_provision_maritime }
		}

		# Office continuity and stakeholder minima survive later route recovery.
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_activated_mulcahy_civil_defence
					has_country_flag = ireland_mulcahy_succession_complete
				}
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_route_leader_activated_costello_coalition
					has_country_flag = ireland_costello_coalition_complete
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_coalition_collapse }
			}
			add_to_variable = { ireland_achievement_mulcahy_costello_continuous_days = constant:ireland.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_mulcahy_costello_continuous_days value = constant:ireland.achievement_office_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_mulcahy_fine_gael_leader
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_costello_taoiseach
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_coalition_cohesion value = ireland_achievement_coalition_cohesion_min compare = less_than } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_coalition_cohesion_min value = ireland_coalition_cohesion }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_movement_unity value = ireland_achievement_movement_unity_min compare = less_than } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_movement_unity_min value = ireland_movement_unity }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_agrarian_cohesion value = ireland_achievement_agrarian_cohesion_min compare = less_than } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_agrarian_cohesion_min value = ireland_agrarian_cohesion }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_compact_trust value = ireland_achievement_compact_trust compare = less_than } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_compact_trust value = ireland_compact_trust }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_veil_stability value = ireland_achievement_veil_stability compare = less_than } }
			set_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_veil_stability value = ireland_veil_stability }
		}

		# Conclusions and failures remain separate from active-party cosmetic changes.
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_full
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_de_valera_reconciliation_concluded
					has_country_flag = ireland_de_valera_emergency_concluded
					has_country_flag = ireland_lemass_developmental_concluded
					has_country_flag = ireland_cosgrave_institutional_concluded
					has_country_flag = ireland_mulcahy_civil_defence_concluded
					has_country_flag = ireland_costello_coalition_concluded
					has_country_flag = ireland_dillon_intervention_concluded
					has_country_flag = ireland_norton_parliamentary_concluded
					has_country_flag = ireland_clann_agrarian_broker_concluded
					has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_vocational_concluded
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_nonhidden_route_conclusion
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_de_valera_stewardship_concluded
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_full
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_de_valera_constitutional_conclusion
		}
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_emergency_repeal_complete } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_emergency_ended }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_fine_gael_government_ready } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_fine_gael_in_government }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_constitutional_stewardship_complete }
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_civil_war_reconciliation_completed
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_paramilitary_influence_removed
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_norton_parliamentary_concluded
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_full
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_norton_led_government
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_parliamentary_social_conclusion
		}
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_clann_founded } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_clann_na_talmhan_founded_dated_path }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_congress_plural_concluded
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_full
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_plural_congress_conclusion
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_ira_concluded
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_full
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_ira_civil_republican_conclusion
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_republican_networks_organised
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_oduffy_corporate_concluded
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_full
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_corporate_institutional_conclusion
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_corporate_movement_reconstructed
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_ailtiri_total_state_concluded
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_full
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_ailtiri_state_conclusion
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_clerical_producer_concluded
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_full
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_clerical_producer_conclusion
		}

		# Northern achievement history requires actual control, stage five integration, final review and the separate core review.
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_core_granted
				has_country_flag = ireland_northern_final_review_passed
				check_variable = { var = ireland_northern_integration_stage value = constant:ireland.northern_integration_stage_complete compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_northern_consent_ledger_passed
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_settle_education_and_religious_safeguards_complete } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_northern_rights_review_passed }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_settle_education_and_religious_safeguards_complete } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_northern_rights_completed }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_maintain_welfare_and_pensions_complete } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_northern_services_completed }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_vet_and_rebuild_policing_complete } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_northern_policing_completed }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_merge_tax_and_debt_administration_complete } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_northern_finance_completed }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = ireland_action_settle_the_demobilised_forces_complete
						has_country_flag = ireland_mission_settle_the_demobilised_forces_full_recorded
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_northern_demobilisation_completed
			}
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_federal_preparation_complete } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_federal_settlement_conclusion }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_northern_unitary_preparation_complete } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_negotiated_unitary_settlement }
		}

		# Accepted obligations are counted from named work, then closed only by full records.
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ireland_action_fulfil_a_compact_obligation_complete
					has_country_flag = ireland_fivefold_obligation_complete
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_action_fulfil_a_compact_obligation_complete NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_obligation_recorded } }
				add_to_variable = { ireland_achievement_accepted_obligation_count = constant:ireland.one }
				set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_obligation_recorded
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_compact_human_rights_passed
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = ireland_achievement_accepted_obligation_count value = constant:ireland.zero compare = greater_than }
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = ireland_mission_fulfil_the_compact_at_five_sites_full_recorded
						has_country_flag = ireland_fivefold_obligation_complete
					}
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_accepted_obligation_failed }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_achievement_accepted_obligation_breached }
				}
				set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_all_accepted_obligations_fulfilled
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_evidence_ledger_complete } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_public_capacities_verified }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_otherworld_concluded
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_full
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_otherworld_conclusion
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_compact_with_otherworld_complete } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_compact_of_two_irelands_conclusion }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_high_king_concluded
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_full
			}
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_high_king_constitutional_path } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_constitutional_high_kingship }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_high_king_sacral_path } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_sacral_crown_conclusion }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_high_king_captured_crown_concluded } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_captive_crown }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ireland_five_provinces_concluded
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_conclusion_full
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_five_provinces_federation_conclusion
			set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_five_provinces_mandates_valid
		}
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_crown_province_convergence_complete } set_country_flag = ireland_achievement_fivefold_high_kingdom_two_irelands_conclusion }
		ireland_update_achievement_history = yes
	}
}

ireland_open_event_gateways = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_initialized NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_closed } }
		set_country_flag = ireland_event_programme_open
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = ireland_event_programme_open }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_closed } clr_country_flag = ireland_event_programme_open }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_window_closed
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_window_active }
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_foreign_reaction_digest_pending
				value = constant:ireland.zero
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_digest_required
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_window_closed
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_window_active }
			check_variable = {
				var = ireland_foreign_reaction_digest_pending
				value = constant:ireland.zero
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = ireland_foreign_reaction_digest_required
	}
}

ireland_tick_h14_bargain_lifecycle = {
	# H14 is a dated, sponsor-dependent negotiation. A prepared handoff cannot
	# remain live after the proposal window or after Britain ceases to be a valid
	# implementing partner. The historical preparation record remains intact;
	# only the unresolved gateway and focus handoff are withdrawn.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ireland_h14_gateway
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_h14_resolved }
			OR = {
				NOT = { ireland_british_partner_valid = yes }
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_closed
				has_country_flag = ireland_route_cleanup
				date > 1941.1.1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { ireland_british_partner_valid = yes }
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_h14_crisis_destroyed
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				date > 1941.1.1
			}
			set_country_flag = ireland_h14_unprepared_delay
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = ireland_h14_crisis_destroyed
		}
		set_country_flag = ireland_h14_resolved
		clr_country_flag = ireland_h14_gateway
		clr_country_flag = ireland_h14_active
		clr_country_flag = ireland_northern_wartime_bargain_ready
		clr_country_flag = ireland_event_152_open
		clr_country_flag = ireland_event_153_open
		clr_country_flag = ireland_event_154_open
		clr_country_flag = ireland_event_155_open
		ireland_recompute_provision = yes
		ireland_recompute_readiness = yes
		ireland_update_settlement_stage = yes
	}
}

ireland_dispatch_event_programme = {
	# The cursor is a bounded durable scan marker. A corrupted or stale value is
	# recovered to the first canonical node before the sparse monthly scan.
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = ireland_event_dispatch_cursor value = constant:ireland.event_dispatch_cursor_reset compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = ireland_event_dispatch_cursor value = constant:ireland.event_dispatch_flavour_last compare = greater_than }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = ireland_event_dispatch_cursor value = constant:ireland.event_dispatch_cursor_reset }
		set_country_flag = ireland_event_cursor_recovered
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = ireland_event_cursor_valid
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ireland_event_programme_open }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ireland_event_dispatch_cooldown value = constant:ireland.zero compare = greater_than } }
			subtract_from_variable = { ireland_event_dispatch_cooldown = constant:ireland.one }
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = ireland_event_dispatch_taken
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_event_dispatch_taken }

					original_tag = IRE
					has_country_flag = ireland_initialized
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_event_seen_100 }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_closed }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_absurd_route_active }
					date > 1937.1.1
					date < 1938.1.1
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = ireland_h01_gateway
						date > 1937.1.1
					}
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = ireland_profile_historical_de_valera
						has_country_flag = ireland_plausible_route_active
						has_country_flag = ireland_absurd_route_active
					}
				}
				set_variable = { var = ireland_event_dispatch_cursor value = 101 }
				country_event = { id = ireland_events.100 }
				set_country_flag = ireland_event_dispatch_taken
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_event_dispatch_taken }

					original_tag = IRE
					has_country_flag = ireland_initialized
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_event_seen_101 }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_route_closed }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_absurd_route_active }
					date > 1937.1.1
					date < 1938.1.1
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = ireland_h01_gateway
						date > 1937.1.1
					}
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = ireland_profile_historical_de_valera
						has_country_flag = ireland_plausible_route_active
						has_country_flag = ireland_absurd_route_active
					}
				}
				set_variable = { var = ireland_event_dispatch_cursor value = 102 }
				country_event = { id = ireland_events.101 }
				set_country_flag = ireland_event_dispatch_taken
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ireland_event_dispatch_taken }

					original_tag = IRE
					has_country_flag = ireland_initialized
